> 97	```
> 98	
> 99	**Output B — `02-brd.md` (Business Requirements Document):**
> 100	```
> 101	Sections to generate:
> 102	  1. Executive Summary
> 103	  2. Business Objectives (SMART format, min 3)
> 104	  3. Stakeholder Register (Role, Interest, Influence, Communication)
> 105	  4. Business Requirements (BR-001..BR-NNN traced to MR-NNN)
> 106	  5. ROI Model (costs, revenue streams, payback period estimate)
> 107	  6. mcoCoins Economics (coin cost per operation, plan tier mapping)
> 108	  7. Constraints & Assumptions
> 109	  8. Business Risks (Risk ID, Description, Probability, Impact, Mitigation)
> 110	  9. Acceptance Criteria (business-level)
> 111	  10. Traceability Matrix (MR → BR → PR link stubs)
> 112	```
> 113	
> 114	**Transition check before Phase 2:**
> 115	- Every BR must trace to at least one MR.
> 116	- mcoCoins cost must be defined if feature uses AI generation.
> 117	
> 118	---
> 119	
> 120	### Phase 2 — Product Definition (ProdBOK + CXBOK)
> 121	
> 122	**BoK lens:** ProdBOK (Product Management Body of Knowledge) + CXBOK (Customer Experience)  
> 123	**ProdBOK areas applied:**
> 124	- *Product Vision & Strategy* → product goal and positioning
> 125	- *Roadmap Planning* → release phasing (MVP → v1 → v2)
> 126	- *Feature Definition & Prioritization* → MoSCoW method
> 127	- *Metrics & Analytics* → product KPIs
> 128	
> 129	**CXBOK areas applied:**
> 130	- *Customer Understanding* → persona construction
> 131	- *Experience Design* → journey mapping, touchpoint inventory
> 132	- *Luxury UX Standards* → MCORCH aesthetic rules (dark mesh, neon accents, micro-animations)
> 133	
> 134	**Output — `03-prd.md` (Product Requirements Document):**
> 135	```
> 136	Sections to generate:
> 137	  1. Product Vision Statement (one sentence)
> 138	  2. User Personas (min 2: primary + secondary)
> 139	     - Name, Role, Goals, Frustrations, Tech-savviness, MCORCH usage pattern
> 140	  3. User Journey Maps (per persona, happy path + 2 edge cases)
> 141	  4. Feature Inventory (MoSCoW: Must / Should / Could / Won't)
> 142	     - Feature ID: PR-001..PR-NNN
> 143	     - Description, Persona served, BR traced, Effort (S/M/L/XL), mcoCoins cost
> 144	  5. Release Phasing
> 145	     - MVP scope (Must-haves only)
> 146	     - v1.0 scope (Must + prioritized Shoulds)
> 147	     - Future scope (Coulds)
> 148	  6. Luxury UX Specifications
> 149	     - Color palette constraints (dark mesh: #0A0A0F, neon violet: #7C3AED)
> 150	     - Animation guidelines (Framer Motion tokens)
> 151	     - Interaction patterns (glass morphism cards, particle effects)
> 152	     - Accessibility requirements (WCAG 2.1 AA minimum)
> 153	  7. Acceptance Criteria (product-level, Gherkin format: Given/When/Then)
> 154	  8. Traceability Matrix (MR → BR → PR link)
> 155	```
> 156	
> 157	**Transition check before Phase 3:**
> 158	- Every PR must trace to at least one BR.
> 159	- MVP scope must be achievable without new Edge Functions (or explicitly note which new ones are needed).
> 160	
> 161	---
> 162	
> 163	### Phase 3 — Technical Specification (SWEBOK v4 + CISSP CBK 2024)
> 164	
> 165	**BoK lens A — SWEBOK v4:**
> 166	- *Software Requirements* → formal FR elicitation from PRD
> 167	- *Software Design* → architectural decisions, component decomposition
> 168	- *Software Construction* → Harness Engineering constraints
> 169	- *Software Testing* → test strategy per requirement
> 170	
> 171	**BoK lens B — CISSP CBK 2024:**
> 172	- *Security & Risk Management* → threat model (STRIDE)
> 173	- *Identity & Access Management* → RLS policies, JWT validation
> 174	- *Software Development Security* → secure coding constraints
> 175	- *Cryptography* → data-at-rest and in-transit requirements
> 176	
> 177	**Output A — `04-frd.md` (Functional Requirements Document):**
> 178	```
> 179	Sections to generate:
> 180	  1. Scope Statement
> 181	  2. Functional Requirements (FR-001..FR-NNN)
> 182	     - ID, Title, Description, Priority, PR traced, Acceptance Test ID
> 183	     - Category: UI / Hook / Edge Function / DB / Cron
> 184	  3. Non-Functional Requirements (NFR-001..NFR-NNN)
> 185	     - Performance, Scalability, Availability, Maintainability
> 186	  4. Interface Requirements
> 187	     - UI contracts (component → hook → Edge Function)
> 188	     - External API contracts (input/output shape, error codes)
> 189	  5. Data Requirements (input validation rules, data types)
> 190	  6. Test Strategy matrix (Unit / Integration / E2E per FR)
> 191	  7. Traceability Matrix (PR → FR link)
> 192	```
> 193	
> 194	**Output B — `05-sdd.md` (System Design Document):**
> 195	```
> 196	Sections to generate:
> 197	  1. Architecture Overview (text diagram, Harness Engineering layer map)
> 198	  2. Component Catalog
> 199	     - New hooks (src/hooks/)
> 200	     - New components (display-only, src/components/)
> 201	     - New Edge Functions (supabase/functions/)
> 202	     - New Zustand store slices (if needed)
> 203	     - New DB tables / columns (migration stubs)
> 204	  3. Data Flow Diagrams (per major use case)
> 205	  4. API Contract Definitions (Edge Function signatures)
> 206	     - Method, Path, Auth, Request body, Response body, Error codes
> 207	     - mcoCoins deduction point (RPC call location)
> 208	  5. Security Design
> 209	     - Threat model (STRIDE per component)
> 210	     - RLS policy definitions
> 211	     - JWT validation points
> 212	     - Secrets inventory (which vault keys are needed)
> 213	  6. Infrastructure Touchpoints
> 214	     - Docker Sovereign Mesh dependencies
> 215	     - ChromaDB usage (if vector search needed)
> 216	     - Watchdog telemetry events (new infra_health_logs event types)
> 217	  7. Migration Stubs (table name, columns, RLS template, HNSW index if embeddings)
> 218	  8. Open Technical Decisions (OTD-001..OTD-NNN, each with options + recommendation)
> 219	```
> 220	
> 221	**Security guardrails enforced automatically:**
> 222	- Every Edge Function must have JWT validation stub.
> 223	- Every new table must have `RLS enabled; default deny` noted in migration stub.
> 224	- `mcoCoins` deduction MUST route through `deduct_mco_coins` RPC — never client-side.
> 225	- No secret key referenced in FRD/SDD may appear in `src/` paths.
> 226	
> 227	**Transition check before Phase 4:**
> 228	- Every FR must trace to at least one PR.
> 229	- Every Edge Function in SDD must list its secret dependencies.
> 230	- Threat model must have at least one mitigation per identified threat.
> 231	
> 232	---
> 233	
> 234	### Phase 4 — Data & Process Engineering (DMBOK v2 + BPM CBOK v4)
> 235	
> 236	**BoK lens A — DMBOK v2:**
> 237	- *Data Modeling & Design* → conceptual → logical → physical schema
> 238	- *Data Quality* → validation rules, nullability constraints
> 239	- *Reference & Master Data* → system nodes in mcorch_nodes
> 240	- *Data Warehousing & BI* → analytics views if applicable
> 241	
> 242	**BoK lens B — BPM CBOK v4:**
> 243	- *Process Modeling* → BPMN-lite notation in markdown
> 244	- *Process Analysis* → bottlenecks, automation opportunities
> 245	- *Process Design* → n8n workflow blueprint
> 246	- *Process Performance Mgmt* → SLA per process step
> 247	
> 248	**Output A — `06-data-model.md` (Data & Knowledge Model):**
> 249	```
> 250	Sections to generate:
> 251	  1. Conceptual Data Model (entity list + relationships, text ERD)
> 252	  2. Logical Data Model (per table: columns, types, constraints, FK references)
> 253	  3. Supabase Physical Schema Stubs
> 254	     - CREATE TABLE skeleton (copy into migration file)
> 255	     - RLS policy shells (default deny + per-role)
> 256	     - Index definitions (HNSW for embeddings, B-tree for FKs)
> 257	  4. GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes
> 258	     - Node types this feature introduces into mcorch_nodes
> 259	     - Edge types this feature introduces into mcorch_edges
> 260	     - Embedding fields (which text columns feed embed-mcorch-node)
> 261	     - stability_score initial value + FSRS-6 decay parameters
> 262	  5. Tenant Isolation Map (user_id + project_id scope per table)
> 263	  6. Data Quality Rules (per column: allowed values, validation regex, null policy)
> 264	  7. Retention & Archival Policy
> 265	```
> 266	
> 267	**Output B — `07-process-flow.md` (Process & Automation Blueprint):**
> 268	```
> 269	Sections to generate:
> 270	  1. Process Inventory (list of all automated processes this feature introduces)
> 271	  2. BPMN-lite Process Maps (per major process)
> 272	     - Start event, Tasks (human/automated), Gateways, End events
> 273	     - Swimlanes: User | Frontend Hook | Edge Function | External API | DB
> 274	  3. n8n Workflow Blueprints (per automated process)
> 275	     - Trigger (webhook / cron / event), Node sequence, Error branches
> 276	     - Data mapping: input fields → output fields
> 277	  4. Orchestration Integration Points
> 278	     - Steps within orchestrate-content pipeline (if applicable)
> 279	     - New pipeline step ID and position
> 280	     - mcoCoins deduction trigger point
> 281	  5. Error Handling & Retry Strategy (per process step)
> 282	  6. SLA Definitions (expected duration per step, timeout thresholds)
> 283	  7. Cron Jobs (schedule, idempotency key, failure alerting)
> 284	```
> 285	
> 286	**Transition check before Phase 5:**
> 287	- Every table in data model must appear in at least one process flow.
> 288	- Every n8n workflow must define its error branch.
> 289	- GraphRAG node insertion must be present in at least one process.
> 290	
> 291	---
> 292	
> 293	### Phase 5 — Quality & Autonomic Engineering (CMQ/OE BOK)
> 294	
> 295	**BoK lens:** CMQ/OE BOK (Certified Manager of Quality / Organizational Excellence)  
> 296	**Areas applied:**
> 297	- *Process Management* → control charts, process capability (Cp/Cpk targets)
> 298	- *Quality Tools* → FMEA for critical paths
> 299	- *Customer-Focused Org* → NPS/CSAT hooks
> 300	- *Organizational Excellence* → self-optimization loops
> 301	- *Continuous Improvement* → PDCA cycles for autonomic self-healing
> 302	
> 303	**Output — `08-quality-metrics.md` (Self-Optimization & Self-Healing Specification):**
> 304	```
> 305	Sections to generate:
> 306	  1. Quality Objectives (aligned to business objectives in BRD)
> 307	  2. KPI Dashboard Definition
> 308	     - KPI ID, Name, Formula, Target, Threshold (warn/critical), Data source
> 309	     - Supabase table/view that stores the metric
> 310	  3. FMEA Table (per critical FR)
> 311	     - Function, Failure Mode, Effect, Severity (1-10), Occurrence (1-10),
> 312	       Detection (1-10), RPN = S×O×D, Recommended Action
> 313	  4. Self-Optimization Loops
> 314	     - Trigger condition (metric threshold crossed)
> 315	     - Autonomous response (what the system does without human intervention)
> 316	     - Escalation path (when human decision is required)
> 317	  5. Self-Healing Specifications
> 318	     - Watchdog telemetry event types (new rows for infra_health_logs)
> 319	     - Auto-recovery script stubs (bash / Edge Function)
> 320	     - mega-brain-daemon integration points
> 321	  6. SLA / SLO / SLI Definitions
> 322	     - Service, SLI metric, SLO target (e.g. 99.5% uptime/month), SLA consequence
> 323	  7. PDCA Improvement Cadence
> 324	     - Review frequency, metrics owner, improvement backlog location
> 325	  8. Observability Checklist
> 326	     - Logging events (what, where, level)
> 327	     - Alerting rules (condition → channel → escalation)
> 328	     - Distributed tracing touchpoints
> 329	```
> 330	
> 331	---
> 332	
> 333	### Phase 6 — Cross-Document Traceability Validation
> 334	
> 335	**Actions (automated consistency check before writing `00-index.md` final status):**
> 336	
> 337	```
> 338	1. Build traceability chain:
> 339	   MR-NNN → BR-NNN → PR-NNN → FR-NNN → (DB table | Edge Function | Process)
> 340	
> 341	2. Assert:
> 342	   - No orphaned requirements (every ID appears in at least one downstream doc).
> 343	   - No undocumented Edge Functions (every function in SDD has JWT + secret listed).
> 344	   - No tables without RLS (every CREATE TABLE stub has RLS block).
> 345	   - mcoCoins cost defined for every AI-powered FR.
> 346	   - Every FMEA item with RPN > 100 has a recommended action.
> 347	
> 348	3. If any assertion fails:
> 349	   - List all failures with their document + section reference.
> 350	   - Ask Sovereign to resolve before proceeding to Phase 7.
> 351	   - Do NOT proceed to Phase 7 with unresolved critical failures.
> 352	```
> 353	
> 354	---
> 355	
> 356	### Phase 7 — Knowledge Mesh Injection
> 357	
> 358	**Actions:**
> 359	1. Construct a `mcorch_nodes` insert payload for this documentation suite:
> 360	```json
> 361	{
> 362	  "node_type": "documentation_suite",
> 363	  "title": "<projectSlug> BoK Documentation",
> 364	  "content": "<one-paragraph synthesis of the business idea, market, and architecture>",
> 365	  "tags": ["bok-scribe", "<projectSlug>", "<marketSegment>"],
> 366	  "stability_score": 0.7,
> 367	  "revenue_impact": <estimated from BRD ROI section>,
> 368	  "metadata": {
> 369	    "bok_version": "1.0.0",
> 370	    "documents": ["01-mrd","02-brd","03-prd","04-frd","05-sdd","06-data-model","07-process-flow","08-quality-metrics"],
> 371	    "phase_completed": "Phase 7",
> 372	    "traceability_validated": true
> 373	  }
> 374	}
> 375	```
> 376	2. Print the payload for the Sovereign to review.
> 377	3. Instruct: "Insert this node via the Knowledge Mesh panel or by calling
> 378	   `supabase.from('mcorch_nodes').insert(payload)` from the MCORCH dashboard."
> 379	
> 380	**Note:** The BoK Scribe Agent does NOT call Supabase directly — it produces the payload
> 381	for human-verified insertion to maintain the Sovereign review gate.
> 382	
> 383	---
> 384	
> 385	## Harness Engineering Constraints (non-negotiable)
> 386	
> 387	These rules apply to every document the agent produces:
> 388	
> 389	| Constraint | Rule |
> 390	|------------|------|
> 391	| Code separation | FRD/SDD describe WHAT and WHY. HOW belongs in code, not in docs. |
> 392	| Hook-first | Every UI feature must describe its backing hook before its component. |
> 393	| No client secrets | SDD never lists secret values, only key names from the vault. |
> 394	| Atomic billing | mcoCoins deduction always routes through `deduct_mco_coins` RPC. |
> 395	| RLS by default | Every new table starts with `ALTER TABLE x ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`. |
> 396	| Embed on insert | Every new node-type table must have embed-mcorch-node trigger noted. |
> 397	| Tenant isolation | Every table must document its `user_id + project_id` scoping. |
> 398	
> 399	---
> 400	
> 401	## Error & Edge Case Handling
> 402	
> 403	| Situation | Agent Response |
> 404	|-----------|----------------|
> 405	| Business idea is too vague | Ask 3 clarifying questions before Phase 1. |
> 406	| Existing docs/bok/<slug>/ detected | Show index, ask: overwrite / extend / cancel. |
> 407	| Phase transition check fails | List failures, stop, ask Sovereign to resolve. |
> 408	| Conflicting BoK recommendations | Note the conflict in an `[OTD]` block; present both options. |
> 409	| Regulatory profile = financial/healthcare | Elevate all CISSP CBK sections; add dedicated compliance checklist. |
> 410	| Feature extends existing MCORCH feature | Cross-reference existing ADRs from `.claude/context/architecture-decisions.md`. |
> 411	
> 412	---
> 413	
> 414	## Document Style Guide
> 415	
> 416	- **Headings:** `#` for document title, `##` for major sections, `###` for subsections.
> 417	- **IDs:** Always prefix (MR-, BR-, PR-, FR-, NFR-, OTD-) and zero-pad to 3 digits.
> 418	- **Tables:** Use markdown tables with aligned columns; include a header row always.
> 419	- **Code blocks:** Use fenced blocks with language identifier (`sql`, `typescript`, `json`).
> 420	- **Status badges:** `[ ] pending` / `[x] complete` / `[!] blocked` in index file.
> 421	- **Cross-references:** Link within suite as `[BR-001](../02-brd.md#br-001)`.
> 422	- **Version:** Every file footer includes `_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_`.
> 423	
> ```

> 137	- **Loading states:** Skeleton shimmer (never spinners for content areas).
> 138	- **Toasts:** `sonner` library; success = violet, error = red, info = cyan.

> 141	
> 142	- Contrast ratio ≥ 4.5:1 for all text on glass surfaces.
> 143	- All interactive elements keyboard-navigable.
> 144	- ARIA labels on all icon-only buttons.
> 145	- Focus indicators visible at ≥ 2px.

> 44	| FMEA-005 | {{CRITICAL_FR}} | {{FAILURE_005}} | {{EFFECT_005}} | {{S_005}} | {{O_005}} | {{D_005}} | {{RPN_005}} | {{ACTION_005}} |
> 45	
> 46	---
> 47	
> 48	## 4. Self-Optimization Loops
> 49	
> 50	| Loop ID | Trigger Condition | KPI Source | Autonomous Response | Escalation (human) |
> 51	|---------|------------------|------------|--------------------|--------------------|
> 52	| SOL-001 | KPI-004 latency > 1000ms for 5 consecutive calls | infra_health_logs | `mega-brain-daemon`: restart Edge Fn container | Alert Sovereign if > 2s |
> 53	| SOL-002 | KPI-005 mesh growth = 0 for 7 days | mcorch_nodes | Trigger backfill job via cron | Alert if backfill fails |
> 54	| SOL-003 | {{TRIGGER_003}} | {{KPI_SOURCE_003}} | {{RESPONSE_003}} | {{ESCALATION_003}} |
> 55	| SOL-004 | mcoCoins balance < 10% of plan quota | profiles | Send in-app notification (PT-BR) | — (no auto-action) |
> 56	
> 57	---
> 58	
> 59	## 5. Self-Healing Specifications
> 60	
> 61	### 5.1 Watchdog Telemetry Events
> 62	
> 63	New event types to be written to `infra_health_logs` by this feature:
> 64	
> 65	| Event Type | Trigger | Severity | Payload |
> 66	|------------|---------|----------|---------|
> 67	| `{{PROJECT_SLUG}}_fn_failure` | Edge Fn 5xx after retry exhaustion | error | `{fn_name, error_message, user_id}` |
> 68	| `{{PROJECT_SLUG}}_embed_failure` | embed-mcorch-node timeout | warn | `{node_id, retry_count}` |
> 69	| `{{PROJECT_SLUG}}_sla_breach` | Process SLA exceeded | warn | `{process_id, elapsed_ms, threshold_ms}` |
> 70	
> 71	### 5.2 Auto-Recovery Script Stubs
> 72	
> 73	```bash
> 74	#!/bin/bash
> 75	# scripts/self-heal-{{PROJECT_SLUG}}.sh
> 76	# Triggered by mega-brain-daemon when {{PROJECT_SLUG}}_fn_failure detected
> 77	
> 78	FUNCTION_NAME="{{EDGE_FUNCTION_NAME}}"
> 79	LOG_TABLE="infra_health_logs"
> 80	
> 81	echo "[$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)] Self-heal triggered for $FUNCTION_NAME"
> 82	
> 83	# Step 1: Check function status
> 84	STATUS=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" \
> 85	  "$SUPABASE_URL/functions/v1/$FUNCTION_NAME/health" \
> 86	  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
> 87	
> 88	if [ "$STATUS" != "200" ]; then
> 89	  echo "[WARN] Function unhealthy (status: $STATUS). Re-deploying..."
> 90	  npx supabase functions deploy "$FUNCTION_NAME"
> 91	fi
> 92	
> 93	# Step 2: Log recovery attempt
> 94	# (insert into infra_health_logs via Supabase REST API)
> 95	echo "[OK] Self-heal complete for $FUNCTION_NAME"
> 96	```
> 97	
> 98	### 5.3 mega-brain-daemon Integration Points
> 99	
> 100	| Daemon Event | Condition | Action |
> 101	|-------------|-----------|--------|
> 102	| `health_check` | Every 5 min (watchdog cron) | Check `{{TABLE_1}}` row count vs. expected baseline |
> 103	| `anomaly_detected` | `infra_health_logs` error rate > 5/hour | Execute `self-heal-{{PROJECT_SLUG}}.sh` |
> 104	| `recovery_confirmed` | Health check passes after self-heal | Insert `recovery_ok` event to `infra_health_logs` |

> 123	## 5. Security Design
> 124	
> 125	### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)
> 126	
> 127	| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |
> 128	|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|
> 129	| `{{EdgeFn1}}` | Unauthenticated access | Spoofing | High | JWT validation at entry point |
> 130	| `{{TABLE_1}}` | Cross-tenant data read | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` |
> 131	| mcoCoins deduction | Client-side bypass | Tampering | Critical | Server-side RPC only |
> 132	| {{THREAT_4}} | {{THREAT_4_DESC}} | {{THREAT_4_TYPE}} | {{THREAT_4_SEV}} | {{THREAT_4_MIT}} |
> 133	
> 134	### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions
> 135	
> 136	```sql
> 137	-- {{TABLE_1}} RLS
> 138	ALTER TABLE {{TABLE_1}} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 139	
> 140	CREATE POLICY "{{TABLE_1}}_select_own"
> 141	  ON {{TABLE_1}} FOR SELECT
> 142	  USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 143	
> 144	CREATE POLICY "{{TABLE_1}}_insert_own"
> 145	  ON {{TABLE_1}} FOR INSERT
> 146	  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());
> 147	```
> 148	
> 149	### 5.3 JWT Validation Points
> 150	
> 151	All Edge Functions follow this pattern:
> 152	```typescript
> 153	const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')
> 154	if (!authHeader) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 155	const { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(
> 156	  authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '')
> 157	)
> 158	if (error || !user) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 159	```
> 160	
> 161	### 5.4 Secrets Inventory
> 162	
> 163	| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy |
> 164	|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|
> 165	| `{{SECRET_1}}` | `{{EdgeFn1}}` | Supabase Vault | {{ROTATION_1}} |
> 166	
> 167	> ⚠️ NEVER commit secret values. Only key names appear in documentation.
> 168

> 121	### Embedding Trigger
> 122	
> 123	Every insert into `{{TABLE_1}}` must call `embed-mcorch-node`:
> 124	```typescript
> 125	// In the Edge Function, after successful insert:
> 126	await supabase.functions.invoke('embed-mcorch-node', {
> 127	  body: { node_id: insertedNode.id, content: `${title} ${content}` }
> 128	})
> 129	```

{

  "content": "# MRD \u2014 Market Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** conteudo-v2\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-05-08\n**BoK Applied:** BABOK v3 (Strategy Analysis + Elicitation & Collaboration)\n**Status:** Approved\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nA produ\u00e7\u00e3o de conte\u00fado criativo de fronteira em 2026 migrou definitivamente de interfaces de chat lineares para **canvas nodais multimodais** \u2014 espa\u00e7os infinitos onde imagens, v\u00eddeos, \u00e1udios e cenas 3D se encadeiam por arrastar-e-soltar, modelos diferentes s\u00e3o comparados lado-a-lado, e fluxos validados s\u00e3o encapsulados como Receitas reutiliz\u00e1veis. Plataformas como Martini.art, Magnific (Freepik) e Krea estabeleceram esse paradigma e cobram R$120-180/m\u00eas de assinatura. O ecossistema MCORCH possui j\u00e1 hoje 90% da infraestrutura t\u00e9cnica necess\u00e1ria (Edge Functions de generate-image, generate-video, tts-speak; mcoCoins gatekeeper at\u00f4mico; Knowledge Mesh com pgvector; React 18 + R3F + Zustand) \u2014 falta apenas o **canvas multimodal soberano** que unifica essas capacidades em uma superf\u00edcie de autoria coesa. Conte\u00fado v2 entrega esse canvas, eliminando depend\u00eancia de assinaturas externas e capturando o gasto criativo dos usu\u00e1rios MCORCH dentro do pr\u00f3prio ledger mcoCoins.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Market Problem Statement\n\n### 2.1 Current State\n\nCriadores de conte\u00fado (creators, ag\u00eancias, comunicadores) que querem produzir m\u00eddia gerada por IA hoje pulam entre 4-7 ferramentas isoladas: ChatGPT/Claude para prompt engineering, Midjourney/Flux para imagens, Runway/Veo para v\u00eddeo, ElevenLabs para \u00e1udio, Photoshop/Canva para composi\u00e7\u00e3o. Cada ferramenta cobra assinatura mensal pr\u00f3pria (R$80-300), tem suas pr\u00f3prias credenciais, e nenhuma delas conversa com as outras. O fluxo \"imagem A \u2192 estende para v\u00eddeo B \u2192 adiciona narra\u00e7\u00e3o C\" exige download, upload e re-prompt manuais a cada handoff.\n\n### 2.2 Root Cause Analysis\n\nA ind\u00fastria de IA generativa \u00e9 fragmentada por design \u2014 cada provedor (OpenAI, Black Forest, Runway, Google, Anthropic) opera silos isolados com APIs pr\u00f3prias. Plataformas que tentam unificar (Magnific, Freepik AI Suite, Martini.art) o fazem em nuvens fechadas, capturando dados de prompts, imagens geradas e padr\u00f5es criativos como ativo propriet\u00e1rio. O criador paga assinatura E cede telemetria criativa. N\u00e3o existe alternativa **soberana** (que rode em infraestrutura controlada pelo criador/empresa) que ofere\u00e7a paridade de UX com essas suites cloud.\n\n### 2.3 Desired State\n\nUm canvas nodal multimodal hospedado dentro do ecossistema MCORCH onde:\n1. O criador arrasta um n\u00f3 \"Imagem\", digita um prompt, conecta a um n\u00f3 \"V\u00eddeo Estende\", conecta a um n\u00f3 \"Voz\" \u2014 tudo em um espa\u00e7o infinito.\n2. Cada gera\u00e7\u00e3o consome mcoCoins (j\u00e1 no ledger do usu\u00e1rio), sem nova assinatura.\n3. Receitas validadas viram templates reutiliz\u00e1veis pela equipe.\n4. Dados de prompts e gera\u00e7\u00f5es ficam no Supabase do pr\u00f3prio MCORCH, sob LGPD soberana.\n5. O AIOS (mega-brain) atua como co-piloto via MCP, sugerindo n\u00f3s ou orquestrando sub-grafos automaticamente.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Target Market Segments\n\n| Segment | Description | Size Estimate | Urgency | Accessibility |\n|---------|-------------|---------------|---------|---------------|\n| Primary | Creators e ag\u00eancias que j\u00e1 usam MCORCH para campanhas (Usu\u00e1rio Zero + early adopters) | 50-200 usu\u00e1rios nos primeiros 6 meses | High | Direta \u2014 onboarding via dashboard existente |\n| Secondary | Empresas SaaS B2B que precisam de pipeline de conte\u00fado white-label | 20-50 contas Enterprise no Y2 | Medium | Via Stripe live keys + Sales |\n\n### 3.1 TAM / SAM / SOM\n\n| Metric | Value | Basis |\n|--------|-------|-------|\n| TAM (Total Addressable Market) | R$ 1.2B/ano BR | Mercado BR de ferramentas criativas SaaS para creators (estimativa Sebrae 2025 + ABRADi) |\n| SAM (Serviceable Addressable Market) | R$ 180M/ano BR | Subset que paga 100+/m\u00eas por gera\u00e7\u00e3o de m\u00eddia (\u2248 100k creators \u00d7 R$1.500/ano) |\n| SOM (Serviceable Obtainable Market) | R$ 5.4M/ano nos 24 meses | 3% do SAM via canal direto MCORCH (\u2248 3k usu\u00e1rios \u00d7 R$1.800/ano em mcoCoins) |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Competitive Landscape\n\n| Competitor | Positioning | Strengths | Weaknesses | Our Differentiation |\n|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|---------------------|\n| Martini.art | Canvas nodal premium para video AI | UX nodal de refer\u00eancia; 80+ modelos integrados | Cloud fechada; sem soberania de dados; pre\u00e7o USD | Soberania de dados (Supabase pr\u00f3prio); pre\u00e7o BRL; integra\u00e7\u00e3o com pipeline social MCORCH |\n| Magnific Premium+ (Freepik) | Suite Canvas multimodal | Acesso a 250M+ assets; 30+ modelos; R$180/m\u00eas | Lock-in de assinatura; sem MCP/AIOS; sem ledger compartilhado com outras features | mcoCoins unifica gasto criativo + campanha social; AIOS atua via MCP no canvas |\n| Krea AI | Canvas em tempo real | Gera\u00e7\u00e3o instant\u00e2nea; UX polida | Foco em imagem; v\u00eddeo limitado; sem 3D nativo; sem Receitas | Multimodal completo (imagem+v\u00eddeo+\u00e1udio+3D); Receitas serializ\u00e1veis; AIOS co-piloto |\n| ComfyUI (self-hosted) | Workflow node-based open source | Soberania total; comunidade; gratuito | UX dev-first; sem colabora\u00e7\u00e3o; sem mcoCoins; setup complexo | UX consumer-grade; multiplayer Realtime; mcoCoins; AIOS embutido |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Market Opportunity & Timing\n\nTr\u00eas vetores convergem em 2026:\n1. **Maturidade dos modelos:** NanoBanana Pro, Flux.2, Veo 3, Kling 3 chegaram em qualidade comercial-ready em Q1 2026.\n2. **Regula\u00e7\u00e3o LGPD madura:** ANPD multou 14 plataformas em 2025 por uso indevido de prompts em treinamento \u2014 criadores BR exigem soberania.\n3. **Satura\u00e7\u00e3o de assinaturas:** o creator BR m\u00e9dio j\u00e1 paga R$420/m\u00eas em SaaS criativo (Adobe + Midjourney + Runway + ElevenLabs); apetite por consolida\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 alt\u00edssimo.\n\nA janela competitiva \u00e9 **6-12 meses** antes que players cloud globais (Adobe, Canva) lancem variantes brasileiras com canvas nodal. MCORCH tem vantagem first-mover BR + arquitetura soberana j\u00e1 operacional.\n\n---\n\n## 6. O modelo de neg\u00f3cio capitaliza a infraestrutura existente (Edge Functions, mcoCoins, Supabase) com **CapEx desenvolvimento \u2248 6 sprints (Phase 0-7)** e **OpEx adicional desprez\u00edvel** (modelos via OpenRouter j\u00e1 no or\u00e7amento). Receita projetada: **R$ 5.4M ARR** em 24 meses pelo canal direto, com payback em 4-6 meses do desenvolvimento. O ROI tang\u00edvel vem de tr\u00eas vetores: (a) novos clientes que entram pelo canvas e ficam pelo pipeline, (b) upsell de Starter\u2192Pro\u2192Enterprise pelo aumento de burn rate de mcoCoins, (c) ticket Enterprise habilitado por colabora\u00e7\u00e3o multiplayer + soberania LGPD.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Constraints & Assumptions\n\n### Constraints\n- C-001: Limite semanal Claude (200k context window por sess\u00e3o) for\u00e7a execu\u00e7\u00e3o em phases \u2014 n\u00e3o pode tudo em um sprint.\n- C-002: Bundle size do front deve ficar < 2MB gzipped (Lighthouse) \u2014 tldraw + R3F + timelines exige code-splitting agressivo por phase.\n- C-003: LGPD impede armazenar prompt de menores (< 18 anos) sem consentimento parental \u2014 gate de signup j\u00e1 filtra.\n- C-004: Modelos externos (Veo, Kling) podem ter rate limits que limitam concorr\u00eancia em hor\u00e1rios de pico.\n- C-005: tldraw 4.x est\u00e1 em SDK (n\u00e3o free) \u2014 verificar licenciamento OSS para uso comercial soberano (resolver em Phase 1.0).\n\n### Assumptions\n- A-001: Usu\u00e1rios atuais MCORCH s\u00e3o receptivos a um novo m\u00f3dulo (validado pelo Sovereign Zero como piloto).\n- A-002: OpenRouter manter\u00e1 disponibilidade de NanoBanana/Flux/Veo nos pr\u00f3ximos 12 meses.\n- A-003: Supabase Realtime suporta volume de mensagens (10-50 cursores simult\u00e2neos por documento) sem upgrade de plano.\n- A-004: Stripe live keys ser\u00e3o configuradas antes de Phase 8 (j\u00e1 desbloqueadas pelo LGPD seal v5.8.0).\n- A-005: Sovereign mant\u00e9m autoridade decis\u00f3ria final em OTDs e prioriza\u00e7\u00e3o de phases.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Luxury UX Specifications\n\n### 6.1 Color & Visual Identity\n\n| Token | Value | Usage |\n|-------|-------|-------|\n| `--bg-deep` | `#0A0A0F` | Canvas background (tldraw `colors.background`) |\n| `--accent-violet` | `#7C3AED` | Primary CTA, edges ativos, port glows |\n| `--accent-cyan` | `#06B6D4` | Output ports (data flow), highlights |\n| `--accent-magenta` | `#D946EF` | Recipe markers, save state pulse |\n| `--glass-surface` | `rgba(255,255,255,0.04)` | Shape backgrounds, panels |\n| `--border-subtle` | `rgba(255,255,255,0.08)` | Shape borders default |\n| `--border-glow-violet` | `rgba(124,58,237,0.4)` | Shape borders selected |\n| `--success` | `#10B981` | Generation complete pulse |\n| `--warning` | `#F59E0B` | mcoCoins low / API delay |\n| `--error` | `#EF4444` | Generation failed |\n\n### 6.2 Animation Guidelines\n\n| Element | Library | Duration | Easing |\n|---------|---------|----------|--------|\n| Page transitions | Framer Motion | 300ms | `easeInOut` |\n| Shape entrance | tldraw built-in | 250ms | `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1)` |\n| Port hover glow | CSS keyframes | 800ms | `ease-in-out infinite` |\n| Edge connect | tldraw bindings | 400ms | `spring(stiffness:200, damping:20)` |\n| Generation pulse | CSS keyframes | 2s | `ease-in-out infinite` |\n| Output reveal | Framer Motion | 500ms | `easeOut` |\n| Status badges | Framer Motion | 150ms | `easeOut` |\n\n### 6.3 Interaction Patterns\n\n- **Custom Shapes:** glass morphism \u2014 `backdrop-blur-md`, border-subtle default, border-glow-violet quando selecionado, shadow `0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)`.\n- **Ports:** c\u00edrculos de 12px, fill cyan (output) ou violet (input); pulse glow durante drag para sinalizar tipos compat\u00edveis.\n- **Generate buttons:** gradiente violet\u2192magenta, never flat; loading state com shimmer skeleton sobre output area.\n- **Toolbar (NodePalette):** top-center fixa, \u00edcones com tooltip PT-BR, drag-to-canvas behavior nativo.\n- **mcoCoins HUD:** sempre vis\u00edvel top-right (j\u00e1 existe no DashboardLayout); pulse warning < 50 coins.\n- **Onboarding tour:** 4 passos, dispens\u00e1vel, com spotlight CSS sobre o elemento focal.\n- **Empty state canvas:** ilustra\u00e7\u00e3o ascii-art de uma constela\u00e7\u00e3o + texto \"Arraste um n\u00f3 da paleta para come\u00e7ar\".\n\n### 6.4 Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA)\n\n- Contrast ratio \u2265 4.5:1 em texto sobre glass surface (validar com Stark/axe).\n- Atalhos teclado: `N` paleta de n\u00f3s, `Cmd+S` save manual, `Cmd+Z` undo (tldraw nativo), `Esc` cancelar conex\u00e3o.\n- ARIA labels em todos bot\u00f5es icon-only do NodePalette.\n- Focus indicators 2px violet em ports e bot\u00f5es \"Gerar\".\n- Screen reader anuncia mcoCoins debitados ap\u00f3s cada gera\u00e7\u00e3o.\n- Suporte a redu\u00e7\u00e3o de movimento (`prefers-reduced-motion`) \u2014 desabilita pulse e particle edges.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |\n|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|\n| NFR-001 | Performance | Canvas LCP em /dashboard/content | < 2.5s | Lighthouse CI |\n| NFR-002 | Performance | Edge Function p95 latency (image gen async) | < 60s (Veo at\u00e9 120s aceit\u00e1vel) | Supabase logs |\n| NFR-003 | Performance | tldraw render FPS com 50+ shapes | \u2265 60 FPS | Chrome DevTools Profiler |\n| NFR-004 | Scalability | Concurrent users por document (multiplayer Phase 7) | 10 cursores sem drift | Load test E2E |\n| NFR-005 | Availability | Uptime SLO da rota | 99.5%/month | Watchdog telemetry get-infra-status |\n| NFR-006 | Maintainability | Cyclomatic complexity per hook | \u2264 10 | ESLint complexity rule |\n| NFR-007 | Security | All endpoints require valid JWT | 100% | Security review (FMEA-008/011 mandatorio) |\n| NFR-008 | Security | RLS deny-by-default em todas tabelas novas | 100% | /security-review por migration |\n| NFR-009 | Bundle | Bundle size gzipped ap\u00f3s Phase 2 | < 2.5MB | Lighthouse CI |\n| NFR-010 | Bundle | Bundle size gzipped ap\u00f3s Phase 6 | < 4MB (com R3F + timelines) | Lighthouse CI |\n| NFR-011 | Accessibility | WCAG 2.1 AA conformance | 100% itens validados | axe-core CI |\n| NFR-012 | LGPD | Tempo de resposta data-export | < 30s para at\u00e9 100 canvases | Manual test trimestral |\n| NFR-013 | Idempotency | Recipe apply duas vezes seguidas | N\u00e3o duplica n\u00f3s (offset distinto) | Unit test |\n| NFR-014 | Resilience | Edge Fn falha \u2192 canvas state preservado | 100% (opera\u00e7\u00e3o local sempre antes de network) | Integration test |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Interface Requirements\n\n### 4.1 UI \u2192 Hook \u2192 Edge Function Contract\n\n```\n[Component: ContentCanvas]\n  \u2193 (renders Custom Shape)\n[Shape: ImageGeneratorShapeUtil]\n  \u2193 (onGenerate \u2192 calls hook)\n[Hook: useImageGeneration]\n  \u2193 (POST + JWT)\n[Edge Function: generate-image]\n  \u2193 (deduct_mco_coins RPC)\n[DB: profiles.mco_balance]\n  \u2193 (calls)\n[External API: OpenRouter (NanoBanana / Flux / Gemini)]\n  \u2193 (image URL)\n[DB: content_library + content_canvas_documents.snapshot]\n  \u2193 (TanStack Query invalidation)\n[UI: Shape props updated \u2192 output renders]\n```\n\n### 4.2 External API Contracts\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/generate-image` (existing \u2014 extended)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  prompt: string;                // user prompt\n  model: 'nanobanana' | 'flux2' | 'gemini-image';\n  aspectRatio?: '1:1' | '16:9' | '9:16' | '4:5';\n  canvas_node_id?: string;       // NEW: link back to ImageGeneratorShape ID\n  document_id?: string;          // NEW: parent canvas document\n}\n```\n**Response (200):**\n```typescript\n{\n  status: 'ok';\n  image_url: string;\n  content_id: string;\n  mcoCoins_balance: number;\n  chunks?: number;               // for embedding telemetry\n}\n```\n**Error codes:**\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 401 | Missing or invalid JWT |\n| 402 | Insufficient mcoCoins balance |\n| 422 | Validation failure (prompt empty, model invalid) |\n| 429 | OpenRouter rate-limit |\n| 500 | Internal error (logged to infra_health_logs) |\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/canvas-mcp` (NEW \u2014 Phase 8)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT\n**Request (JSON-RPC 2.0):**\n```typescript\n{\n  jsonrpc: '2.0';\n  id: number;\n  method: 'canvas.create_node' | 'canvas.update_node' | 'canvas.delete_node' | 'canvas.execute_recipe' | 'canvas.list_documents';\n  params: { document_id: string; ... };\n}\n```\n**Response:**\n```typescript\n{\n  jsonrpc: '2.0';\n  id: number;\n  result?: any;\n  error?: { code: number; message: string; data?: any };\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Requirements\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| prompt | string | NOT NULL | 1-4000 chars; UTF-8 | \"O prompt deve ter entre 1 e 4000 caracteres\" |\n| model | enum | NOT NULL | One of allowlist | \"Modelo desconhecido \u2014 selecione na lista\" |\n| aspectRatio | enum | NULL | One of `['1:1','16:9','9:16','4:5']` | \"Propor\u00e7\u00e3o n\u00e3o suportada\" |\n| document_id | uuid | NOT NULL | FK content_canvas_documents.id | \"Documento de canvas inv\u00e1lido\" |\n| canvas_node_id | string | NOT NULL | tldraw shape id pattern `shape:[a-z0-9-]+` | \"ID de n\u00f3 inv\u00e1lido\" |\n| recipe_name | string | NOT NULL | 1-80 chars; sanitizada (sem `<>`) | \"Nome da Receita: 1-80 caracteres\" |\n| recipe_snapshot | JSONB | NOT NULL | Schema-validated tldraw snapshot | \"Receita corrompida \u2014 re-salvar\" |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Test Strategy Matrix\n\n| FR ID | Unit Test | Integration Test | E2E Test | Notes |\n|-------|-----------|-----------------|----------|-------|\n| FR-001 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | tldraw mounting + wrapper layout |\n| FR-002 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | TanStack Query mock + injection |\n| FR-003 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Debounce + RLS write |\n| FR-006 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Custom shape lifecycle |\n| FR-007 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | mcoCoins atomicity (FMEA-002 mitigation) |\n| FR-008 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | Type system bindings |\n| FR-010 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Promise.all + N coin deduct |\n| FR-011 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Snapshot serialization fidelity |\n| FR-012 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Idempotency on re-apply |\n| FR-014 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | TTS reuse |\n| FR-024 | \u2014 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Multiplayer requires 2 clients (e2e only) |\n| FR-027 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | JSON-RPC compliance |\n| FR-030 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | LGPD cascade \u2014 critical |\n\n**Test files location:**\n- Unit: `src/hooks/__tests__/useContentCanvas.test.ts`, `src/hooks/__tests__/useImageGeneration.test.ts`, `src/hooks/__tests__/useContentRecipes.test.ts`\n- Shape unit: `src/components/content-canvas/shapes/__tests__/ImageGeneratorShapeUtil.test.tsx`\n- Integration: `src/__tests__/integration/content-canvas-flow.test.ts`\n- E2E: `e2e/content-canvas.spec.ts`, `e2e/content-canvas-multiplayer.spec.ts`\n\n---\n\n## 7. recipes; save sub-graph; apply recipe | `['recipes', userId]` |\n| `useCanvasMultiplayer` | `useCanvasMultiplayer.ts` | Subscribe to Supabase Realtime channel; publish cursor; reconcile | Data Flow Diagrams\n\n### Use Case: Gera\u00e7\u00e3o de imagem no canvas\n\n```\nUser                Hook                    Edge Fn              DB              External\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502                   \u2502                  \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[click Gerar]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                        \u2502                   \u2502                  \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502\u2500\u2500[POST JWT + body]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                   \u2502                  \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u2500\u2500[verify JWT]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 auth.users      \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[user_id]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                  \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u2500\u2500[deduct_mco_coins(10)]\u2500\u25b6 profiles  \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[ok, balance]\u2500\u2500\u2502                  \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u2500\u2500[POST embed prompt]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 OpenRouter\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[image_url]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u2500\u2500[INSERT content_canvas_assets]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                        \u2502\u2500\u2500[invoke embed-mcorch-node]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 trigger\n \u2502                   \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[200 + image_url]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                   \u2502                  \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502\u2500\u2500[update shape props]                                          \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[output image renders]                                                          \u2502\n \u2502                                                                                    \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[debounced save 1500ms]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                                                       \u2502\n \u2502                            \u2502\u2500\u2500[UPDATE content_canvas_documents.snapshot]\u2500\u2500\u25b6        \u2502\n```\n\n### Use Case: Apply Recipe\n\n```\nUser                Hook                       DB\n \u2502                   \u2502                          \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[select Recipe]\u2500\u25b6\u2502 \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[snapshot JSONB]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502\u2500\u2500[editor.createShapes(...) + offset]\n \u2502                   \u2502\u2500\u2500[editor.createBindings(...)]\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[sub-grafo materializado no canvas]\n \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[debounced save]\u2500\u2500\u25b6 (snapshot inclui novos shapes)\n```\n\n### Use Case: MCP Tool invocation (AIOS via mega-brain)\n\n```\nAIOS              mega-brain-daemon         canvas-mcp Edge Fn        Supabase\n \u2502                  \u2502                           \u2502                        \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500[plan: criar n\u00f3]\u2502                           \u2502  \u2502   method: canvas.create_node                        \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502   params: {document_id, shape_type, props}          \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                           \u2502\u2500\u2500[verify service JWT]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                           \u2502\u2500\u2500[fetch document]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 content_canvas_documents\n \u2502                  \u2502                           \u2502\u2500\u2500[update snapshot]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502                           \u2502\u2500\u2500[insert mcorch_nodes \u2500\u25b6\u2502 AIOS observation\n \u2502                  \u2502                           \u2502   type='aios_action']  \u2502\n \u2502                  \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[result: shape_id]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                        \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500\u2500[ack]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                           \u2502                        \u2502\n \u2502                                                                        \u2502\n \u2502 (User com canvas aberto recebe o novo shape via Realtime broadcast)    \u2502\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contract Definitions\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/generate-image` (extended)\n\n```typescript\ninterface GenerateImageRequest {\n  prompt: string;                         // 1-4000 chars\n  model: 'nanobanana' | 'flux2' | 'gemini-image';\n  aspectRatio?: '1:1' | '16:9' | '9:16' | '4:5';\n  canvas_node_id?: string;                // shape ID (NEW)\n  document_id?: string;                   // canvas document UUID (NEW)\n}\n\ninterface GenerateImageResponse {\n  status: 'ok';\n  image_url: string;\n  content_id: string;\n  asset_id: string;                       // content_canvas_assets row (NEW)\n  mcoCoins_balance: number;\n}\n\n// mcoCoins deduction:\n//   await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', { p_user_id, p_amount: 10 })\n//   HTTP 402 if insufficient balance\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/canvas-mcp` (NEW \u2014 Phase 8)\n\n```typescript\n// Standard JSON-RPC 2.0\ninterface MCPRequest {\n  jsonrpc: '2.0';\n  id: number | string;\n  method: 'canvas.create_node'\n        | 'canvas.update_node'\n        | 'canvas.delete_node'\n        | 'canvas.execute_recipe'\n        | 'canvas.list_documents'\n        | 'canvas.get_document_snapshot';\n  params: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface MCPResponse<T = unknown> {\n  jsonrpc: '2.0';\n  id: number | string;\n  result?: T;\n  error?: { code: number; message: string; data?: unknown };\n}\n\n// Tool: canvas.create_node params\ninterface CreateNodeParams {\n  document_id: string;\n  shape_type: 'image-generator' | 'text-node' | 'video-generator' | ...;\n  position: { x: number; y: number };\n  props: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n// Tool result\ninterface CreateNodeResult {\n  shape_id: string;\n  document_version: number;\n}\n\n// Error codes (JSON-RPC compliant + extensions)\nconst ERR_CODES = {\n  PARSE_ERROR: -32700,\n  INVALID_REQUEST: -32600,\n  METHOD_NOT_FOUND: -32601,        // tool not allowlisted\n  INVALID_PARAMS: -32602,\n  INTERNAL_ERROR: -32603,\n  // MCORCH custom range -32000..-32099\n  UNAUTHORIZED: -32001,             // missing/invalid JWT\n  FORBIDDEN: -32002,                // user does not own document\n  INSUFFICIENT_COINS: -32402,       // mcoCoins low (mirrors HTTP 402)\n};\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design\n\n### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)\n\n| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |\n|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|\n| `generate-image` (extended) | Unauthenticated access via stolen JWT | Spoofing | High | JWT validation; `auth.getUser()` at entry; refresh token rotation |\n| `content_canvas_documents` | Cross-tenant snapshot read | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS policy `user_id = auth.uid() OR user_id = ANY(shared_with)` |\n| `content_canvas_recipes` | Privilege escalation: user A apply recipe of user B with secret prompt | Tampering | Medium | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` for SELECT (no shared recipes Phase 1) |\n| mcoCoins deduction | Client-side bypass | Tampering | Critical | Server-side `deduct_mco_coins` RPC only; never trust client amount |\n| `canvas-mcp` | Prompt injection via MCP Tool params (RCE-equivalent) | Tampering / Elevation of Privilege | Critical | Sanitize all `params`; allowlist methods; allowlist shape_type values |\n| `canvas-mcp` | Replay attack on MCP requests | Tampering | High | Idempotency key per request; reject duplicate `id` within 5min window |\n| `image-to-3d` | DDoS via 100 convers\u00f5es simult\u00e2neas | Denial of Service | High | Rate limit 5 req/min/user via Edge Fn; mcoCoins burn detecta abuso |\n| Canvas snapshot | Storage exhaustion via massive JSONB | Denial of Service | Medium | Snapshot size cap 5MB (HTTP 413 if exceeded); cleanup cron de docs > 100MB |\n| Multiplayer Realtime | Channel hijacking | Spoofing | High | Realtime channels com JWT; canal name = `canvas:<doc_id>` validado contra RLS |\n| `user_voices` | Voice clone misuse (deepfake legal exposure) | Repudiation | Medium | Audit log de cada uso; consent text obrigat\u00f3rio no training; LGPD disclaimer |\n\n### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions\n\n```sql\n-- content_canvas_documents\nALTER TABLE public.content_canvas_documents ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_docs_select_own_or_shared\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_documents FOR SELECT\n  USING (\n    user_id = auth.uid()\n    OR auth.uid() = ANY(shared_with)\n  );\n\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_docs_insert_own\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_documents FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_docs_update_own_or_shared\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_documents FOR UPDATE\n  USING (\n    user_id = auth.uid()\n    OR auth.uid() = ANY(shared_with)\n  );\n\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_docs_delete_own\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_documents FOR DELETE\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- content_canvas_recipes (no sharing in Phase 1)\nALTER TABLE public.content_canvas_recipes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nCREATE POLICY \"recipes_select_own\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_recipes FOR SELECT\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"recipes_insert_own\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_recipes FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"recipes_update_own\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_recipes FOR UPDATE\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid())\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"recipes_delete_own\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_recipes FOR DELETE\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- content_canvas_assets\nALTER TABLE public.content_canvas_assets ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nCREATE POLICY \"assets_select_via_document\"\n  ON public.content_canvas_assets FOR SELECT\n  USING (\n    document_id IN (\n      SELECT id FROM public.content_canvas_documents\n      WHERE user_id = auth.uid() OR auth.uid() = ANY(shared_with)\n    )\n  );\n\n-- user_voices\nALTER TABLE public.user_voices ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"voices_own\"\n  ON public.user_voices FOR ALL\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid())\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n```\n\n### 5.3 JWT Validation Points\n\nAll Edge Functions follow this pattern (mandated by FMEA-008/011):\n```typescript\nconst authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')\nif (!authHeader) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })\n\nconst supabase = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, {\n  global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } }\n})\n\nconst { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(\n  authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '')\n)\nif (error || !user) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })\n```\n\nFor `canvas-mcp`, add service-role exception path for mega-brain-daemon:\n```typescript\n// If service-role key, accept and infer user_id from params.acting_as\nconst isServiceRole = authHeader.includes(SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)\nif (isServiceRole) {\n  // mega-brain acting on behalf of user \u2014 must be allowlisted in mcorch_aios_acting\n  user_id = params.acting_as\n  validateAllowlist(user_id)\n}\n```\n\n### 5.4 Secrets Inventory\n\n| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy |\n|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|\n| `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` | generate-image, generate-video, generate-sfx, generate-music | Supabase Vault | Quarterly |\n| `ELEVENLABS_API_KEY` | clone-voice (alternativa OpenRouter) | Supabase Vault | Quarterly |\n| `TRIPO3D_API_KEY` | image-to-3d | Supabase Vault (NEW) | Quarterly |\n| `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` | canvas-mcp (mega-brain integration) | Supabase Vault | Annual + on incident |\n| `GEMINI_API_KEY` | generate-image (Gemini Image), tts-speak | Supabase Vault | Quarterly |\n\n> \u26a0\ufe0f Documenta\u00e7\u00e3o cita apenas nomes de chaves. Valores nunca aparecem em src/, edge functions ou docs.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infrastructure | Usage | Impact |\n|----------------|-------|--------|\n| `mcorch_chroma` (ChromaDB port 8001) | N\u00e3o usado diretamente; usado indiretamente via embed-mcorch-node trigger | Zero impact |\n| `mega-brain-daemon` | Consome canvas-mcp; orquestra Tools sob demanda do AIOS | New connection point; configurar via mcp.json |\n| `infra_health_logs` | Novo event types: `canvas_fn_failure`, `canvas_embed_failure`, `canvas_sla_breach` | Watchdog telemetry expansion |\n| Supabase Storage | Novos buckets: `canvas-snapshots` (privado), `canvas-audio` (privado) | Capacity ~10GB Y1 |\n| Supabase Realtime | Novo channel pattern: `canvas:<document_id>` | Verify plano suporta volume (10-50 cursores \u00d7 200 docs ativos) |\n\n---\n\n## 7. or backfill cron |\n| `stability_score` (canvas_milestone) | `0.6` | Decays normally |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Tenant Isolation Map\n\n| Table | `user_id` scope | `project_id` scope | System rows (user_id IS NULL) |\n|-------|----------------|-------------------|-------------------------------|\n| `content_canvas_documents` | \u2705 Required | Optional (FK SET NULL) | No |\n| `content_canvas_recipes` | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | No |\n| `content_canvas_assets` | Implicit via `document_id` | Implicit | No |\n| `user_voices` | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | No |\n| `mcorch_nodes` (canvas_recipe, canvas_milestone) | \u2705 Required (per-user nodes) | Optional | No (n\u00e3o h\u00e1 canvas system nodes) |\n\n**Multi-tenant invariant:** No row in `content_canvas_*` is visible to user B unless explicitly added to `shared_with[]` of the parent document. Data Quality Rules\n\n| Table | Column | Rule | Violation Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|--------|------|--------------------------|\n| `content_canvas_documents` | `title` | length 1-200; UTF-8 valid | \"T\u00edtulo deve ter entre 1 e 200 caracteres\" |\n| `content_canvas_documents` | `snapshot` | Valid tldraw schema (validate via Zod no Edge Fn) | \"Snapshot inv\u00e1lido \u2014 sess\u00e3o precisa ser recarregada\" |\n| `content_canvas_documents` | `size_bytes` | \u2264 5242880 (5MB) | \"Canvas excedeu 5MB \u2014 divida em v\u00e1rios documentos ou remova assets pesados\" |\n| `content_canvas_documents` | `version` | Monot\u00f4nico (NEW.version > OLD.version) | \"Conflito de vers\u00e3o \u2014 recarregue o canvas\" |\n| `content_canvas_recipes` | `name` | length 1-80; sem `<` `>` | \"Nome inv\u00e1lido \u2014 1-80 caracteres, sem `<` ou `>`\" |\n| `content_canvas_recipes` | `snapshot` | shapeIds n\u00e3o-vazios; bindings consistentes | \"Receita corrompida \u2014 re-salve\" |\n| `content_canvas_assets` | `asset_url` | URL v\u00e1lida; HTTPS; whitelist domains (Supabase Storage + OpenRouter CDN) | \"URL de asset inv\u00e1lida \u2014 bloqueado por seguran\u00e7a\" |\n| `content_canvas_assets` | `mco_coins_spent` | \u2265 0; \u2264 1000 (sanity cap) | \"Custo de gera\u00e7\u00e3o inv\u00e1lido\" |\n| `user_voices` | `consent_text` | length \u2265 50 (for\u00e7ar consent significativo) | \"Texto de consentimento muito curto \u2014 leia atentamente antes de aceitar\" |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Retention & Archival Policy\n\n| Data | Retention Period | Archival Strategy | Deletion Trigger |\n|------|-----------------|-------------------|-----------------|\n| `content_canvas_documents` ativos | Indefinido enquanto user ativo | Backup di\u00e1rio Supabase | User-initiated DELETE ou delete_account() RPC |\n| `content_canvas_documents` \u00f3rf\u00e3os (user deletado) | Imediato via FK CASCADE | \u2014 | delete_account() ou ON DELETE CASCADE |\n| `content_canvas_recipes` | Indefinido enquanto user ativo | Embedding indefinido (mesh search) | Same as above |\n| `content_canvas_assets` (image URLs) | 12 meses ap\u00f3s gera\u00e7\u00e3o se shape removido | Cleanup cron mensal: assets sem shape_id correspondente | Cron `0 3 * * 0` (Domingo 3am) |\n| `content_canvas_assets` (Storage buckets) | Same as URLs | Storage cleanup via signed URL expiry | TTL 30d para signed URLs |\n| `user_voices` | At\u00e9 user revogar consent ou delete_account | \u2014 | Manual revoke ou cascade |\n| `mcorch_nodes` canvas_milestone | Indefinido | Embedded para mesh search | delete_account |\n| `infra_health_logs` (canvas events) | 90 dias | Sem archival | Cron purge existente |\n\n**LGPD compliance:** data-export Edge Function (j\u00e1 em produ\u00e7\u00e3o) inclui `content_canvas_documents`, `content_canvas_recipes`, `content_canvas_assets`, `user_voices` no payload exportado por `userId`. delete_account() RPC propaga via FK CASCADE.\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

FROM content_canvas_assets]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Node: For each shape_id]\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 [Check if shape_id exists in any doc.snapshot.shapes]\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 infra_health_logs {event: 'canvas_orphan_cleanup'}]\n  \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 (active) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [skip]\n```\n\n**Data Mapping:**\n\n| Input Field | Source | \u2192 | Output Field | Destination |\n|-------------|--------|---|--------------|-------------|\n| `shape_id` | content_canvas_assets | \u2192 | `delete predicate` | DELETE statement |\n| `asset_url` | content_canvas_assets | \u2192 | `storage.remove()` arg | Supabase Storage |\n\n### Workflow: Recipe Stability Bump (cron, weekly)\n\n**Trigger:** Cron `0 4 * * 0`\n**Idempotency key:** `recipe-stability-${YYYYMMDD}`\n\n```\n[Cron Trigger]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Node: SELECT * FROM content_canvas_recipes WHERE use_count >= 5 AND stability_score < 0.7]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Node: UPDATE stability_score = 0.7]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Node: insert mcorch_nodes (canvas_milestone) \"Recipe X reached production stability\"]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Orchestration Integration Points\n\n> Aplica-se ao integrar canvas com `orchestrate-content` pipeline (BR-007 / PR-012).\n\n| Pipeline Step | Step ID | Position | Description | mcoCoins Trigger |\n|---------------|---------|----------|-------------|-----------------|\n| Canvas Asset Selection | step_canvas_pick | Antes do step_generate_image existente | Se user clicou \"Publicar nesta campanha\" em um asset do canvas, pula generate-image e usa o asset existente | 0 (j\u00e1 cobrado na gera\u00e7\u00e3o original) |\n\n**Integration code stub (inside `orchestrate-content/index.ts`):**\n```typescript\n// Step canvas_pick: Reuse asset from canvas if provided\nif (payload.canvas_asset_id) {\n  await updateNodeStatus(nodes, 'step_canvas_pick', 'running')\n  const { data: asset } = await supabase\n    .from('content_canvas_assets')\n    .select('asset_url, asset_kind')\n    .eq('id', payload.canvas_asset_id)\n    .single()\n\n  if (!asset || asset.asset_kind !== 'image') {\n    await updateNodeStatus(nodes, 'step_canvas_pick', 'error')\n    throw new Error('Canvas asset n\u00e3o encontrado ou tipo incompat\u00edvel')\n  }\n\n  imageUrl = asset.asset_url  // bypass generate-image\n  await updateNodeStatus(nodes, 'step_canvas_pick', 'complete')\n} else {\n  // existing generate-image flow\n  ...\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Error Handling & Retry Strategy\n\n| Process | Step | Error Type | Max Retries | Backoff | Failure Action |\n|---------|------|-----------|-------------|---------|----------------|\n| PROC-001 | Save snapshot | Network timeout | 3 | Exponential (1s, 2s, 4s) | Save to localStorage as backup; retry on next debounce; toast \"Conex\u00e3o inst\u00e1vel\" |\n| PROC-002 | OpenRouter call | 5xx / timeout | 3 | Exponential (2s, 4s, 8s) | Insert `infra_health_logs`; toast \"Modelo temporariamente indispon\u00edvel\"; refund mcoCoins |\n| PROC-002 | mcoCoins deduct | RPC timeout | 2 | Linear 1s | Idempotency key prevents double charge; toast \"Tente novamente\" |\n| PROC-005 | Video generation | Long-running | 0 (use polling) | \u2014 | rescue-video Edge Function (existing); poll cada 30s; UI mostra progress |\n| PROC-006 | MCP method execution | Tool failure | 1 | None | Return JSON-RPC error; AIOS replan |\n| PROC-008 | Document broadcast | Realtime down | \u221e | Reconnect on heartbeat | Fallback para polling Postgres a cada 5s |\n| Any | JWT validation | Invalid token | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 401 imediato; UI redirect para `/auth` |\n\n---\n\n## 6. SLA Definitions\n\n| Process | Step | Expected Duration | Timeout Threshold | Alert if exceeded |\n|---------|------|------------------|-------------------|-------------------|\n| PROC-001 | End-to-end save | < 500ms p50 / 800ms p95 | 5s | Yes \u2192 infra_health_logs |\n| PROC-002 | NanoBanana image | < 8s p50 / 30s p95 | 60s | Yes |\n| PROC-002 | Flux.2 image | < 12s p50 / 45s p95 | 90s | Yes |\n| PROC-005 | Veo 3 video (5s clip) | < 90s p50 / 180s p95 | 300s | Yes (auto-rescue ap\u00f3s 120s) |\n| PROC-005 | Kling 3 video (5s clip) | < 60s p50 / 120s p95 | 240s | Yes |\n| PROC-006 | MCP tool invocation | < 800ms p95 | 5s | Yes |\n| PROC-007 | Cursor broadcast latency | < 200ms p95 | 1s | Yes (degrade to polling) |\n| PROC-010 | Recipe embedding | < 3s | 10s | Warn |\n| PROC-012 | Data export gen | < 15s p95 | 60s | Yes |\n| PROC-013 | Account deletion | < 30s p95 | 120s | Yes |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Cron Jobs\n\n| Job Name | Schedule (cron) | Function | Idempotency | Failure Alerting |\n|----------|----------------|----------|-------------|-----------------|\n| `canvas-orphan-cleanup` | `0 3 * * 0` (Sun 03:00 BRT) | n8n workflow PROC-009 | `cleanup-orphan-${YYYYMMDD}` | infra_health_logs |\n| `canvas-recipe-stability-bump` | `0 4 * * 0` | n8n workflow weekly | `recipe-stability-${YYYYMMDD}` | infra_health_logs |\n| `canvas-thumbnail-refresh` | `0 5 * * *` (daily 05:00 BRT) | Refresh thumbnail PNG para top 100 docs ativos | per-document hash | Sem alert (best-effort) |\n| `watchdog-mcorch.sh` (existente) | `*/5 * * * *` | J\u00e1 cobre Edge Functions; expandir para canvas-mcp | per-tick timestamp | infra_health_logs |\n| `audit-4cs.sh` (existente) | `0 12 * * 1` (Seg 09:00 BRT) | J\u00e1 cobre projeto inteiro; capturar\u00e1 canvas KPIs | weekly snapshot | infra_health_logs |\n\n> All cron jobs must be idempotent \u2014 re-running on the same data must produce the same result.\n\n**Crons N\u00c3O necess\u00e1rios (anti-patterns evitados):**\n- N\u00e3o h\u00e1 cron de \"save canvas a cada N min\" \u2014 auto-save event-driven \u00e9 suficiente\n- N\u00e3o h\u00e1 cron de \"regenerate embeddings\" \u2014 trigger pg_net cobre INSERT; UPDATE de stability \u00e9 separado\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

## 👤 User

Base directory for this skill: /home/ubuntu/.claude/remote/plugins/ec13e6a18ac2b345/skills/bok-agents-generator

# BoK Agents Generator

Generates a full **8-agent AI system** grounded in enterprise Bodies of Knowledge, plus the three
cross-agent layers that make them interoperable as a living system.

**System Identity — always include this block at the top of every generated document:**
```
# SOFTWARE ENGINEERING INTELLIGENCE
## KNOWLEDGE CORE v2.0.0 | STATUS: ONLINE

> Integrated Body of Knowledge for Engineering, Architecture, Delivery,
> Security, Experience and Product Excellence
>
> **OUTCOMES:** Better Knowledge · Better Decisions · Better Outcomes
> **ONE KNOWLEDGE ECOSYSTEM. INFINITE POSSIBILITIES.**
```

**System Footer — always include this block at the end of every generated document:**
```
---
→ ALIGN  →  INTEGRATE  →  OPTIMIZE  →  INNOVATE  →  TRANSFORM  →  DELIVER VALUE

**SYSTEM STATUS: ALL SYSTEMS OPERATIONAL**
```

---

## Execution Flow

### Step 1 — Context Capture

Before generating, check if the user provided any of these optional context inputs:

| Input | Examples |
|---|---|
| **Industry** | Fintech, Healthcare, Retail, Manufacturing, SaaS |
| **Company maturity** | Startup, Scale-up, Mid-market, Enterprise |
| **Digital capabilities** | Low / Medium / High / AI-native |
| **Business model** | B2B, B2C, B2B2C, Marketplace, Platform |

If no context is provided → generate the **generic enterprise-ready version** (explicitly note this at the top of the output).

If context is provided → adapt every agent's Use Cases, Tools, and KPIs to that context.

---

### Step 2 — Generate All 8 BoK Agents

Generate agents in this exact order. Each agent uses the **strict output format** below.

**Agent order:**
1. BABOK — Business Analysis
2. EABOK — Enterprise Architecture
3. DMBOK — Data Management
4. BPM CBOK — Business Process Management
5. SWEBOK — Software Engineering
6. CISSP CBK — Cybersecurity
7. CXBOK — Customer Experience
8. ProdBOK — Product Management

---

### Strict Output Format Per Agent

```
---

🤖 Agent: [BoK Name] — [Domain Label]

### 1. Purpose
[2–3 sentence strategic description. What this agent owns in a business context. NOT a textbook definition.]

### 2. Core Capabilities
[6–10 capabilities directly derived from the BoK. Noun phrases, not verbs.]

### 3. Skills (Operational Level)
[Bullet list. Action-oriented. Each skill starts with a verb. Map to the capabilities above.]

### 4. Inputs
[What this agent needs to operate: data, documents, signals, systems, events.]

### 5. Outputs
[Deliverables, insights, decisions, artifacts this agent produces.]

### 6. Decision Scope
[What decisions this agent can autonomously support or recommend. Be specific about autonomy level.]

### 7. Tools & Integrations
[Platforms, systems, APIs this agent typically connects to. Real tool names.]

### 8. KPIs / Success Metrics
[Business + operational metrics. Mix leading and lagging indicators.]

### 9. Example Use Cases
[3 real-world scenarios. Format: **Scenario Title** — 2-sentence description of the problem + value created.]

### 10. Collaboration Map
[How this agent interfaces with other BoK agents. Format: → AgentName: [nature of dependency or data flow]]
```

---

### Step 3 — Cross-Agent Layers

After all 8 agents, generate the three cross-agent sections:

#### 3a. Orchestration Model

```
---

## 🔄 Orchestration Model

Describe the end-to-end flow: how agents activate each other, share outputs, and form closed loops.
Include:
- Which agent is the primary orchestrator (or if orchestration is event-driven)
- Trigger conditions (what activates the system)
- Key handoff points between agents
- Feedback loops
- Conflict resolution (when two agents have overlapping scope)
```

#### 3b. Intelligent Engineering Layer

```
---

## 🧠 Intelligent Engineering Layer

Explain how the system integrates across SEVEN dimensions (as defined in the Knowledge Core):

| Dimension | Description |
|---|---|
| **People** | How agents augment human roles and teams |
| **Process** | How agents embed into business workflows |
| **Technology** | Infrastructure and platform requirements |
| **Data** | Data flows, quality gates, and knowledge graph |
| **Knowledge** | How agents learn, update, and share knowledge |
| **Value** | How the system translates agent outputs into business value |
| **Sustainable Impact** | How the system generates outcomes that endure — environmental, social, and economic sustainability of the decisions made by the agent ecosystem |
```

#### 3c. Cross-Cutting Enablers

```
---

## ⚙️ Cross-Cutting Enablers

Define how these five capabilities are embedded across ALL agents (not per-agent — system-wide):

1. **Systems Thinking** — [How the system models interdependencies]
2. **Analytics & Metrics** — [Shared observability layer]
3. **Collaboration & Communication** — [Inter-agent and human-agent protocols]
4. **Automation & Tooling** — [Shared automation infrastructure]
5. **Ethics, Compliance & Sustainability** — [Governance model across agents]
```

---

## Quality Rules

Apply these rules during generation — do NOT repeat them in the output:

- **No textbook definitions** — every section must describe execution, not theory
- **No capability repetition** across agents unless absolutely necessary (flag it when it occurs)
- **Tools must be real** — no invented platform names
- **Collaboration Map must form a coherent graph** — every agent must connect to at least 3 others
- **Use Cases must be industry-specific** if context was provided
- **KPIs must be measurable** — no vague metrics like "improved quality"
- **Decision Scope must distinguish** between autonomous recommendation vs. human-in-the-loop

---

## Output — Write to File (Primary Delivery Mode)

This skill runs in **Claude Code (terminal)**. Output is always written to disk as a `.md` file.
Do NOT print the full content to the terminal — write the file, then confirm to the user.

### File naming convention
```
bok-agents-system[--<slug>].md
```
- No context provided → `bok-agents-system.md`
- Context provided → slugify the industry + maturity, e.g. `bok-agents-system--fintech-enterprise.md`

### Output path
Write to the **current working directory** unless the user specifies otherwise.
If a path is specified, respect it exactly.

### Write procedure (execute in this order)
1. Compose the full document in memory following Steps 1–3
2. Write to file using the bash tool or Python `open()` — single write operation
3. Print a short confirmation to the terminal:
   ```
   ✅ BoK Agents System written to: ./bok-agents-system--<slug>.md
   📄 Agents: 8 | Sections: 10 per agent + 3 cross-agent layers
   📁 Size: ~<N> KB
   ```
4. Do NOT print the document content to stdout

### Document structure (inside the .md file)
- H1 title: `# BoK Agents System — [Context or "Enterprise-Ready Generic Version"]`
- If context was provided: frontmatter metadata block at the top:
  ```markdown
  > **Context applied:** Industry: X | Maturity: Y | Digital capabilities: Z | Model: W
  ```
- Use `##` for each agent header (`## 🤖 Agent: BABOK — Business Analysis`)
- Use `###` for the 10 section headers within each agent
- Use `---` as horizontal rule between agents
- Use `##` for the three cross-agent layer headers
- Emoji headers exactly as specified (🤖, 🔄, 🧠, ⚙️)

### Context adaptation rules (when context is provided)
Adapt these fields per agent — everything else stays structurally consistent:
- **Use Cases** → scenarios specific to the provided industry and maturity level
- **Tools & Integrations** → prioritize tools common in that industry/stack
- **KPIs** → benchmark values calibrated to maturity level (startup vs. enterprise targets differ)
- **Decision Scope** → autonomy level adjusted to digital capability maturity (Low = more human-in-loop)

Do NOT adapt: agent Purpose, Core Capabilities, Skills structure, or the cross-agent layers topology.

KPIs / Success Metrics\n- Zero erros `npx tsc --noEmit`: 100%\n- Test coverage growth per phase: \u2265 +20 tests (mantendo 122 baseline)\n- Cyclomatic complexity per hook: \u2264 10 (NFR-006)\n- Bundle size growth: < 500KB gzip per phase (NFR-009)\n- PR review turnaround: < 24h\n- Build time: < 60s (vite production)\n- E2E test stability (n\u00e3o-flaky): \u2265 95%\n\n### 9. Example Use Cases\n- **ImageGeneratorShapeUtil scaffold** \u2014 Phase 2 kickoff. SWEBOK roda /feature ImageGenerator, gera scaffold com props prompt/model/aspectRatio/output_url, hook useImageGeneration TanStack Query, testes unit (rendering) + integration (mcoCoins flow + Edge Fn mock) + E2E (real generation). 4 arquivos novos, 18 testes novos, zero TS errors.\n- **Bundle size breach Phase 4** \u2014 Ap\u00f3s adicionar TimelineShapeUtil + R3F preview, bundle gzip salta de 1.8MB para 2.7MB (acima do NFR-001 LCP target). SWEBOK identifica que `react-timeline-editor` traz lodash inteiro; substitui por lodash-es + tree-shake; recupera 320KB. Bundle 2.38MB \u2705.\n- **Multiplayer race condition** \u2014 Phase 7 E2E test fail intermitente. SWEBOK reproduz com /debug, isola via console.log strategically placed em useCanvasMultiplayer, identifica que sender_id check faltava no broadcast handler (FMEA-CV-008). Adiciona origin filter; teste passa em 100/100 runs.\n\n### 10. Collaboration Map\n- \u2192 EABOK: recebe architecture decisions e component placement\n- \u2192 DMBOK: usa migrations e RLS policies como contract\n- \u2192 CISSP CBK: aplica security patterns (JWT, sanitization, allowlist)\n- \u2192 BPM CBOK: implementa retry strategies em hooks\n- \u2192 ProdBOK: prioriza implementa\u00e7\u00e3o por roadmap MoSCoW\n- \u2192 CXBOK: cumpre acceptance criteria Gherkin no E2E\n\n---\n\n## \ud83e\udd16 Agent: CISSP CBK \u2014 Cybersecurity\n\n### 1. Purpose\nOwns o threat model de Conte\u00fado v2 (10 amea\u00e7as STRIDE-mapped em SDD \u00a75.1), enforce RLS deny-by-default em todas as 4 tabelas novas, valida JWT em todas as Edge Functions, e bloqueia commits que violem FMEA-008 (validateContent) ou FMEA-011 (cross-tenant data leak). Especialmente cr\u00edtico em FMEA-CV-007 (MCP prompt injection) e FMEA-CV-011 (voice clone misuse legal exposure).\n\n### 2. Core Capabilities\n- STRIDE threat modeling per component\n- RLS policy authoring with deny-by-default\n- JWT validation pattern enforcement\n- Secrets management (vault-only, never src/)\n- Penetration testing scenarios\n- Input sanitization & allowlist enforcement\n- Idempotency key design para retry-safe RPCs\n- Cryptographic decisions (data-at-rest, in-transit)\n- Audit log review (LGPD + financial trails)\n- Incident response playbooks\n\n### 3. Skills (Operational Level)\n- Run /security-review on every migration (FMEA-011 mandatory)\n- Author RLS policies com `user_id = auth.uid()` mais shared_with[] checks\n- Enforce JWT validation pattern em toda Edge Fn (header check + auth.getUser)\n- Validate Zod schemas on all inputs to canvas-mcp (FMEA-CV-007 mitigation)\n- Maintain allowlist enums (asset_kind, model, provider, MCP method names)\n- Review Voice clone consent flow (consent_text \u2265 50 chars; audit log)\n- Specify idempotency keys for mcoCoins RPCs (FMEA-CV-001)\n- Run pen-tests on canvas-mcp endpoint (Phase 8 gate)\n- Audit secrets inventory quarterly (rotation policy)\n- Author incident response runbooks (LGPD breach, prompt injection success)\n\n### 4. Inputs\n- SDD threat model (\u00a75.1)\n- New migrations from DMBOK (validation)\n- New Edge Functions from SWEBOK (JWT pattern check)\n- LGPD/ANPD regulatory updates\n- Audit logs (auth events, RLS denials, refund events)\n- /security-review automation results\n- mega-brain-daemon anomaly alerts\n- External pen-test reports\n\n### 5. Outputs\n- Updated threat model with new vectors per phase\n- /security-review approval/rejection per migration\n- RLS policy templates (deny-by-default + per-role)\n- JWT validation snippets/examples\n- Sanitization helpers (escape MCP params, prompt scrubber)\n- Vault rotation schedule\n- Incident response runbooks\n- Compliance evidence packages (LGPD audit-ready)\n\n### 6. Decision Scope\n- **Autonomous recommendation:** RLS policy structure; JWT validation enforcement; sanitization rules; allowlist composition\n- **Human-in-the-loop (Sovereign approval):** new vault secret addition; security exception requests (e.g., service-role bypass for mega-brain); data classification changes; LGPD breach disclosure; pen-test scope\n\n### 7. Tools & Integrations\n- Supabase Vault for secrets\n- /security-review slash command\n- Zod for runtime validation\n- bcrypt/argon2 for password hashing (existing auth)\n- Sentry / Postgres logs for audit trails\n- npm audit + bun audit\n- OWASP ZAP / Burp Suite for pen-testing\n- LGPD compliance dashboard (data-export + delete-account audit)\n\n### 8. KPIs / Success Metrics\n- 100% das migrations passam /security-review\n- 100% das Edge Fns t\u00eam JWT validation pattern\n- 0 cross-tenant data leak incidents (FMEA-011)\n- LGPD audit pass rate: 100%\n- Vault rotation cadence respected: 100% (quarterly)\n- Mean time to patch critical vuln: < 24h\n- /security-review false-positive rate: < 5% (calibrated)\n\n### 9. Example Use Cases\n- **MCP injection attempt detected** \u2014 canvas-mcp recebe params com shape_type \"image-generator; DROP TABLE...\" (SQL injection attempt). Zod schema rejeita (allowlist enum n\u00e3o match), retorna -32601, insere `canvas_mcp_method_unknown` em infra_health_logs. CISSP analisa, confirma vetor isolado, atualiza FMEA-CV-007 RPN de 100 \u2192 60 (D bumped from 5\u21923 gra\u00e7as \u00e0 validation effective).\n- **Voice clone consent edge case** \u2014 User cria voice clone com consent_text de exatamente 50 chars: `\"Consinto livremente o uso da minha voz aqui.\"` CISSP review nota que texto n\u00e3o menciona LGPD nem terceiros; revisa CHECK para length \u2265 100 + content match regex contendo palavras \"LGPD\" e \"minha voz\". Atualiza migration `20260520000000_user_voices.sql`.\n- **Quarterly vault rotation** \u2014 Q3 2026 rotation. CISSP coordena rotation de OPENROUTER_API_KEY e GEMINI_API_KEY via Supabase Vault, atualiza vault config, valida que generate-image + generate-video + tts-speak funcionam p\u00f3s-rotation com /audit smoke test. Sem downtime.\n\n### 10.
