handoff de PR catalog priorizado para implementation\n- \u2192 BPM CBOK: integra SLA performance em product KPIs\n- \u2192 DMBOK: solicita data quality views para dashboards de produto\n- \u2192 CISSP CBK: gate de release para compliance LGPD pr\u00e9-Enterprise\n\n---\n\n## \ud83d\udd04 Orchestration Model\n\n**Primary orchestrator pattern: hybrid event-driven + Sovereign-as-conductor.**\n\n### Trigger conditions (system activation)\n1. **New requirement (Sovereign-driven):** Sovereign verbalize idea \u2192 BABOK opens MR/BR draft \u2192 triggers cascade.\n2. **Telemetry anomaly (event-driven):** infra_health_logs critical event \u2192 mega-brain-daemon \u2192 invokes BPM CBOK + CISSP CBK depending on event type.\n3. **Phase milestone (calendar-driven):** sprint-priorities.md phase complete \u2192 ProdBOK initiates retrospective + next phase planning.\n4. **Customer signal (external):** NPS response, support ticket, Stripe event \u2192 CXBOK or ProdBOK lead investigation.\n5. **Compliance review (calendar):** trimestral LGPD audit \u2192 CISSP CBK leads, pulls data from DMBOK + ProdBOK.\n\n### Key handoff points\n```\n[New Idea]\n  \u2192 BABOK (MR/BR draft + ROI)\n    \u2192 ProdBOK (MoSCoW positioning + release phasing)\n      \u2192 CXBOK (persona impact + journey mapping)\n        \u2192 EABOK (architecture fit + ADR)\n          \u251c\u2192 DMBOK (data model + RLS)\n          \u251c\u2192 BPM CBOK (process spec + SLA)\n          \u2514\u2192 CISSP CBK (threat model + RLS validation)\n            \u2192 SWEBOK (implementation)\n              \u2192 CISSP CBK (/security-review gate)\n                \u2192 SWEBOK (deploy)\n                  \u2192 CXBOK (NPS instrumentation)\n                    \u2192 ProdBOK (telemetry \u2192 next-phase input)\n                      \u2192 BABOK (ROI re-projection)\n[Closed loop]\n```\n\n### Feedback loops\n- **Quality loop:** SWEBOK delivery \u2192 CISSP CBK security review \u2192 CXBOK accessibility/UX critique \u2192 ProdBOK release gate. Cycle \u2264 1 sprint.\n- **Economics loop:** ProdBOK telemetry (mcoCoins ARPU) \u2192 BABOK ROI re-projection \u2192 BABOK risk register update \u2192 ProdBOK pricing recalibration. Cycle trimestral.\n- **Knowledge loop:** Every significant decision/event \u2192 mcorch_nodes (canvas_milestone, canvas_aios_action) \u2192 AIOS via canvas-mcp \u2192 enriches future BABOK requirement quality. Continuous.\n- **Self-healing loop:** infra_health_logs critical \u2192 mega-brain-daemon \u2192 SOL-001..007 self-optimization \u2192 BPM CBOK process spec update if pattern. Cycle 5min watchdog tick.\n\n### Conflict resolution (overlapping scope)\n| Conflict | Resolution |\n|----------|------------|\n| BABOK quer feature X; ProdBOK considera fora do roadmap | ProdBOK arbitrates with telemetry; BABOK can elevate to Sovereign if disagreement persists |\n| EABOK prop\u00f5e refactor amplo; SWEBOK reporta high cost | OTD timebox 48h; Sovereign decides if cost > value |\n| DMBOK schema decision vs. CISSP RLS rejection | CISSP CBK has veto on security; DMBOK redesigns schema |\n| CXBOK premium animation vs. SWEBOK bundle budget | EABOK arbitrates with NFR-009/010; usually CXBOK adapts (lazy-load, code-split) |\n| BPM CBOK new cron vs. ops constraint (existing crons) | BPM CBOK consolidates; never overlapping cron windows |\n\n---\n\n## \ud83e\udde0 Intelligent Engineering Layer\n\n| Dimension | Description |\n|---|---|\n| **People** | 8 agents augmenting Sovereign Gabriel + Engineering executors (Claude/Antigravity) + future Designers/Compliance roles. Each agent reduces cognitive load by owning a specific BoK lens; Sovereign retains authority on decisions marked \"human-in-the-loop\". Sovereign Zero (Gabriel-as-creator) \u00e9 o user research source prim\u00e1rio em B2B2C. |\n| **Process** | Agents embed em sprint cadence (sprint-priorities.md), git workflow (commits cite PR-NNN), session protocol (/handson + /handoff), e cron telemetria (watchdog 5min, audit-4cs semanal). Cada phase tem entry gate (BoK validated) e exit gate (release notes + KPI baseline). |\n| **Technology** | Stack: React 18 + Vite + TypeScript strict + tldraw 4.x + R3F + Zustand + TanStack Query (frontend); Supabase Postgres + Edge Fns Deno + Realtime + Storage + pgvector (backend); ChromaDB + mega-brain-daemon (mesh); Docker Sovereign Mesh + Oracle Cloud (infra); MCP protocol (AIOS bridge). 5 novas Edge Fns + 4 novas tabelas em Conte\u00fado v2. |\n| **Data** | Knowledge Mesh \u00e9 a mem\u00f3ria compartilhada entre agentes \u2014 cada decis\u00e3o significativa vira n\u00f3 (canvas_milestone, canvas_recipe, canvas_aios_action) com embedding 768d. Schema-first via DMBOK migrations; data quality enforced via CHECK + RLS + validation hooks. LGPD data flow rastreado por column (Section 6 of 06-data-model). |\n| **Knowledge** | Agents leem do BoK suite (`docs/bok/conteudo-v2/`) + ADRs (`.claude/context/architecture-decisions.md`) + sprint history (HANDOFF.md). Aprendem via canvas_milestone insertions; consolidam via /audit weekly + /handoff per session. Embedding pipeline (OpenRouter text-embedding-3-small + averaging) garante semantic search. /bok-scribe \u00e9 o ponto de update do BoK; /bok-agents-generator \u00e9 o ponto de update deste sistema. |\n| **Value** | M\u00e9tricas claras: mcoCoins ARPU R$ 60\u2192120 (BO-002), 60% activation (BO-001), NPS \u2265 50 (BO-004), 5 Enterprise contas (BO-005), 100% LGPD (QO-005). Translated em receita projetada R$ 720k Y1 \u2192 R$ 5.4M ARR Y2. Cada agent contribui para subset desses KPIs (Section 8 de cada agent). |\n| **Sustainable Impact** | **Environmental:** mcoCoins desincentiva gera\u00e7\u00e3o desperdi\u00e7ada (vs. flat-rate Magnific que encoraja overuse); modelos quantizados favorecidos em radar tecnol\u00f3gico. **Social:** soberania LGPD protege creators BR contra captura de dados criativos por plataformas estrangeiras; voice clone consent_text obrigat\u00f3rio (FMEA-CV-011) previne misuse. **Economic:** payback em 4-6 meses; ROI 7.5x Y1; substitui 4 assinaturas externas (R$ 420/m\u00eas creator BR m\u00e9dio) com gasto centralizado em ecossistema soberano BR. |\n\n---\n\n## \u2699\ufe0f Cross-Cutting Enablers\n\n### 1. Systems Thinking\nO Knowledge Mesh (`mcorch_nodes` + `mcorch_edges` com pgvector + GraphRAG) modela explicitamente as interdepend\u00eancias entre cada decis\u00e3o, requirement, processo, schema e milestone. Cada agent insere `canvas_milestone` ou `canvas_aios_action` quando toma decis\u00e3o significativa, e qualquer agent pode descobrir via embedding similarity quais decis\u00f5es passadas se relacionam. Isso previne reinventar solu\u00e7\u00f5es e detecta conflicting decisions cedo. ConstellationMatrix em `/dashboard/constellation` visualiza essa rede em 3D para Sovereign \u2014 visibilidade f\u00edsica do tecido decisional do projeto.\n\n### 2. Analytics & Metrics\nCamada de observabilidade compartilhada via:\n- `infra_health_logs` (telemetria por evento, 8 novos canvas_* event types)\n- `get-infra-status` Edge Function (real-time HUD)\n- `audit-4cs.sh` (snapshot semanal Context/Connections/Capabilities/Cadence)\n- `mcorch_nodes` (KPI-007 mesh growth rastre\u00e1vel)\n- Logging events padronizados (Section 8.1 quality-metrics) \u2014 todos agents emitem nesse formato\n- Distributed tracing spans (canvas.generate_image.*, canvas.recipe.*, canvas.mcp.*) \u2014 Section 8.3\n\nProdBOK l\u00ea os 12 KPIs trimestral; DMBOK l\u00ea data quality KPIs continuamente; CISSP l\u00ea audit logs continuamente. Mesmo formato, mesmo dashboard (`/dashboard/admin/canvas-ops` Y2).\n\n### 3. Collaboration & Communication\n- **Inter-agent (formal):** via Knowledge Mesh nodes (canvas_milestone), HANDOFF.md (sprint memory), `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (priority backlog), ADRs em `.claude/context/architecture-decisions.md`. Cada agent l\u00ea esses 4 antes de agir.\n- **Inter-agent (real-time):** via canvas-mcp Tools (Phase 8) \u2014 AIOS pode invocar agents via JSON-RPC; agents publicam a\u00e7\u00f5es em mcorch_nodes que outros consomem via Realtime broadcast.\n- **Human-agent (Sovereign):** /handson at session start (brief sprint-aware), /handoff at session end (seal commit + milestone), AskUserQuestion para decis\u00f5es \"human-in-the-loop\". PT-BR para narrativa; English para code/logs.\n- **Human-agent (Designers/Compliance):** via skills design suite (`/design-system`, `/design-handoff`) e compliance evidence packages (CISSP outputs).\n\n### 4. Automation & Tooling\nShared infrastructure:\n- **Slash commands:** /bok-scribe, /bok-agents-generator, /audit, /level-up, /debug, /edge, /migration, /feature, /handson, /handoff, /security-review, /design-* \u2014 cada agent owns subset; descobre relev\u00e2ncia via skill descriptions\n- **Crons:** watchdog `*/5min`, auto-publish `*/5min`, audit-4cs `seg 09:00`, ast-ingest `daily 06:00`, canvas-orphan-cleanup `dom 03:00` (novo), canvas-recipe-stability-bump `dom 04:00` (novo)\n- **Edge Functions:** orchestrate-content, generate-image, generate-video, tts-speak, embed-mcorch-node, get-infra-status, rescue-video (existing) + canvas-mcp, clone-voice, generate-sfx, generate-music, image-to-3d (novos)\n- **CI/CD:** Lighthouse CI (NFR-001/009), ESLint, vitest (CI on PR), Playwright (CI weekly)\n- **Local dev:** /handson para session start, /handoff para session seal, /sync-mobile-branch para integra\u00e7\u00e3o de bra\u00e7os paralelos (OpenCode/MiniMax sensory-mcp + gamificacao-mcoCoins)\n\n### 5. Ethics, Compliance & Sustainability\nGovernance model:\n- **LGPD enforcement:** DMBOK + CISSP co-own. data-export + delete_account RPC + cascade FK validados E2E mensal. user_voices consent_text \u2265 50 chars. Audit log de uso voice clone.\n- **Direitos autorais IA:** ProdBOK + CXBOK adicionam disclaimer \"Gerado por IA\" em assets exportados. User assume responsibility por prompt content (Termos de Uso atualizados pre-launch).\n- **mcoCoins ethics:** atomic deduct via RPC (FMEA-CV-001 mitigation); refund autom\u00e1tico em failure (FMEA-CV-010); idempotency keys; transpar\u00eancia via mco_transactions audit. Sem dark patterns (\"just R$ 5 more!\").\n- **Voice clone misuse prevention:** FMEA-CV-011 RPN 189 \u2014 most critical. CISSP enforces consent + audit + LGPD disclaimer; ProdBOK deprice em 100 coins (alto custo desencoraja casual abuse).\n- **AI sustainability:** EABOK technology radar prioriza modelos quantizados / efficient (NanoBanana lighter > Flux pesado quando paridade visual permitir); BPM CBOK previne retry storms; ProdBOK desincentiva burn (vs. unlimited plans Magnific).\n- **Ecosystem sustainability (BR):** soberania de dados como compromisso p\u00fablico; substitui\u00e7\u00e3o efetiva de 4 SaaS estrangeiros; capital criativo flui dentro de ecossistema BR; receita reinvestida no MCORCH.\n\n---\n\u2192 ALIGN  \u2192  INTEGRATE  \u2192  OPTIMIZE  \u2192  INNOVATE  \u2192  TRANSFORM  \u2192  DELIVER VALUE\n\n**SYSTEM STATUS: ALL SYSTEMS OPERATIONAL**\n"
}

> 83	### FASE 2 — Database Foundation
> 84	**Migrações Supabase (sem conflito com existentes):**
> 85	
> 86	```sql
> 87	-- 1. trends (Viral Miner)
> 88	CREATE TABLE trends (
> 89	  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 90	  workspace_id UUID REFERENCES workspaces(id),
> 91	  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id),
> 92	  platform TEXT NOT NULL,        -- tiktok/instagram/youtube/etc
> 93	  niche TEXT NOT NULL,           -- slug do nicho
> 94	  title TEXT NOT NULL,
> 95	  thumbnail_url TEXT,
> 96	  score INTEGER DEFAULT 0,       -- 0-100 viral score
> 97	  velocity TEXT DEFAULT 'stable', -- rising/peak/declining/stable
> 98	  engagement_count BIGINT DEFAULT 0,
> 99	  view_count BIGINT DEFAULT 0,
> 100	  trend_metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
> 101	  eta_to_peak_hours INTEGER,
> 102	  source_url TEXT,
> 103	  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
> 104	  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
> 105	);
> 106	-- RLS: default deny, SELECT para owner
> 107	
> 108	-- 2. canvas_projects
> 109	CREATE TABLE canvas_projects (
> 110	  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 111	  workspace_id UUID REFERENCES workspaces(id),
> 112	  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) NOT NULL,
> 113	  name TEXT NOT NULL,
> 114	  description TEXT,
> 115	  canvas_data JSONB DEFAULT '{"nodes":[],"edges":[]}',
> 116	  status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',  -- active/archived
> 117	  total_executions INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
> 118	  last_execution_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
> 119	  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
> 120	  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
> 121	);
> 122	
> 123	-- 3. canvas_nodes (executions)
> 124	CREATE TABLE canvas_executions (
> 125	  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 126	  project_id UUID REFERENCES canvas_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 127	  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) NOT NULL,
> 128	  node_data JSONB NOT NULL,      -- snapshot do node no momento da execução
> 129	  model TEXT,
> 130	  prompt TEXT,
> 131	  output_url TEXT,
> 132	  credits_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
> 133	  status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending/running/done/failed
> 134	  error_message TEXT,
> 135	  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
> 136	);
> 137	
> 138	-- 4. affiliate_products (Radar)
> 139	CREATE TABLE affiliate_products (
> 140	  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 141	  network TEXT NOT NULL,         -- amazon/hotmart/monetizze/eduzz/mercadolivre
> 142	  external_id TEXT NOT NULL,
> 143	  name TEXT NOT NULL,
> 144	  image_url TEXT,
> 145	  commission_pct NUMERIC(5,2),
> 146	  estimated_epc NUMERIC(10,2),
> 147	  profitability_score INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
> 148	  niche TEXT,
> 149	  is_trending BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
> 150	  product_metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
> 151	  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
> 152	  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
> 153	  UNIQUE(network, external_id)
> 154	);
> 155	
> 156	-- 5. affiliate_links (tracking)
> 157	CREATE TABLE affiliate_links (
> 158	  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 159	  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) NOT NULL,
> 160	  product_id UUID REFERENCES affiliate_products(id),
> 161	  short_code TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
> 162	  destination_url TEXT NOT NULL,
> 163	  utm_params JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
> 164	  click_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
> 165	  conversion_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
> 166	  revenue_attributed NUMERIC(12,2) DEFAULT 0,
> 167	  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
> 168	);
> 169	```

> 1	# 🔀 VIRALmind → MCORCH Integration — Walkthrough
> 2	
> 3	## Summary
> 4	
> 5	Successfully integrated the VIRALmind creative intelligence platform into the enterprise MCORCH ecosystem. This includes the **Viral Miner** (trend mining), **Canvas Studio** (generative AI pipeline), and foundational **Affiliate Radar** modules.
> 6	
> 7	---
> 8	
> 9	## What Was Built
> 10	
> 11	### 1. Database Schema (4 Migrations)
> 12	
> 13	All tables use `vm_` namespace prefix to avoid collision with existing `mcorch_` tables.
> 14	
> 15	| Migration | Tables | Purpose |
> 16	|---|---|---|
> 17	| `20260514040000` | `vm_niches` + 5 ENUMs | Niche catalog with 18 seeded entries |
> 18	| `20260514040100` | `vm_trends`, `vm_user_trends` | Trend detection & user favorites |
> 19	| `20260514040200` | `vm_canvas_projects`, `vm_canvas_assets`, `vm_canvas_executions`, `vm_canvas_recipes` | Canvas Studio pipeline |
> 20	| `20260514040300` | `vm_affiliate_products`, `vm_affiliate_links`, `vm_affiliate_clicks` | Affiliate Radar tracking |
> 21	
> 22	> [!IMPORTANT]
> 23	> All tables enforce **default-deny RLS**. Edge Functions use `service_role` for writes.
> 24	
> 25	### 2. Edge Functions
> 26	
> 27	#### `fetch-trends`
> 28	- Multi-source: Apify → RapidAPI → DB cache fallback
> 29	- Supports all platforms: TikTok, Instagram, YouTube, YouTube Shorts, Twitter
> 30	- Auto-deduplicates trends via `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`
> 31	
> 32	#### `canvas-execute`
> 33	- **All providers available:**
> 34	  - OpenAI (DALL·E 3, GPT Image 1)
> 35	  - Replicate (FLUX 1.1 Pro, FLUX Schnell, SDXL)
> 36	  - OpenRouter (DALL·E 3 routed)
> 37	  - Higgsfield
> 38	- Credit-based billing (deducts `mco_balance` from profiles)
> 39	- Auto-upload to `canvas-assets` Supabase Storage bucket
> 40	- Full execution tracking with status machine (running → success/failed)
> 41	
> 42	### 3. React Pages & Hooks
> 43	
> 44	#### Hooks
> 45	- [useViralTrends.ts](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useViralTrends.ts) — Trends, niches, mining, saving
> 46	- [useCanvasStudio.ts](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useCanvasStudio.ts) — Projects CRUD, execution, assets, provider catalog
> 47	
> 48	#### Pages
> 49	- [ViralMinerPage.tsx](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/pages/ViralMinerPage.tsx) — `/dashboard/miner`
> 50	- [CanvasStudioPage.tsx](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/pages/CanvasStudioPage.tsx) — `/dashboard/canvas`
> 51	- [CanvasEditorPage.tsx](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/pages/CanvasEditorPage.tsx) — `/dashboard/canvas/:id` (ReactFlow)
> 52	
> 53	### 4. Infrastructure Updates
> 54	
> 55	- **App.tsx**: 3 new lazy-loaded routes
> 56	- **DashboardSidebar.tsx**: 2 new nav items (Viral Miner + Canvas Studio)
> 57	- **DashboardLayout.tsx**: Canvas Editor added to full-canvas mode + breadcrumbs
> 58	- **Supabase types**: Regenerated with all `vm_*` tables (45 new type references)
> 59	
> 60	---
> 61	
> 62	## Production Verification
> 63	
> 64	````carousel
> 65	![Viral Miner — Live on production](/home/ubuntu/.gemini/antigravity/brain/ff3577d6-ecd6-40e9-9b8b-be0c503a79c8/viral_miner_verified_1778733243569.png)
> 66	<!-- slide -->
> 67	![Canvas Studio — Live on production](/home/ubuntu/.gemini/antigravity/brain/ff3577d6-ecd6-40e9-9b8b-be0c503a79c8/canvas_studio_success_1778732969991.png)
> 68	````
> 69	
> 70	- ✅ `bun run build` — zero errors
> 71	- ✅ `npx tsc --noEmit` — zero errors
> 72	- ✅ Nginx serves from `dist/` directly (auto-deploy)
> 73	- ✅ Both pages confirmed live at `login.mcorch.com`
> 74	
> 75	---
> 76	
> 77	## Bug Fix Applied
> 78	
> 79	**Radix Select crash** — `<SelectItem value="">` causes a React error in Radix UI. Fixed by using `value="all"` sentinel values for the "All Niches" and "All Scores" placeholder options.
> 80	
> 81	---
> 82	
> 83	## Remaining (Sprint 4)
> 84	
> 85	1. **Affiliate Radar** — Evolve existing `AffiliatesPage.tsx` to use `vm_affiliate_*` tables
> 86	2. **MCOPM Widget** — Dashboard metric showing Monetized Content Per Month
> 87	3. **Edge Function** — `process-affiliate-link` for click tracking and attribution
> 88	
> ```

> 107	  }, []);
> 108	
> 109	  const handleAddNode = (type: string) => {

> 111	      ? reactFlowRef.current.screenToFlowPosition({ x: window.innerWidth / 2, y: window.innerHeight / 2 })
> 112	      : { x: 250, y: 250 };
> 113

> 127	    setNodes((nds) => [...nds, newNode]);

> 131	  };
> 132	
> 133	  const handleSave = () => {

> 136	    saveGraph.mutate(

> 141	      }

> 143	  };
> 144	
> 145	  const handleExecute = () => {
> 146	    if (!selectedNode || !projectId) return;

> 148	    // Update node data first

> 150	      nds.map((n) =>

> 156	
> 157	    executeNode.mutate(

> 206	  return (

> 213	        {/* Top Bar */}
> 214	        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 border-b border-border/50 bg-card/80 backdrop-blur z-10">

> 315	                {currentProviderModels.find((m) => m.id === nodeModel)?.description && (
> 316	                  <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mt-1">
> 317	                    {currentProviderModels.find((m) => m.id === nodeModel)?.description}
> 318	                  </p>
> 319	                )}
> 320	              </div>
> 321	
> 322	              {/* Prompt */}
> 323	              <div>
> 324	                <label className="text-xs font-medium mb-1 block text-muted-foreground">Prompt</label>
> 325	                <Textarea
> 326	                  placeholder="A cinematic shot of a cyberpunk city at sunset..."
> 327	                  value={nodePrompt}
> 328	                  onChange={(e) => setNodePrompt(e.target.value)}
> 329	                  rows={4}
> 330	                  className="bg-background/50"
> 331	                />
> 332	              </div>
> 333	
> 334	              {/* Negative Prompt */}
> 335	              <div>
> 336	                <label className="text-xs font-medium mb-1 block text-muted-foreground">Negative Prompt (optional)</label>
> 337	                <Input
> 338	                  placeholder="blurry, low quality, watermark..."
> 339	                  value={nodeNegPrompt}
> 340	                  onChange={(e) => setNodeNegPrompt(e.target.value)}
> 341	                  className="bg-background/50"
> 342	                />
> 343	              </div>
> 344	
> 345	              {/* Cost badge */}
> 346	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-background/30 rounded-lg p-3">
> 347	                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Cost</span>
> 348	                <Badge className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 gap-1">
> 349	                  <Coins className="h-3 w-3" /> {currentModelCost} mcoCoins
> 350	                </Badge>
> 351	              </div>
> 352	
> 353	              {/* Execute */}
> 354	              <Button
> 355	                onClick={handleExecute}
> 356	                disabled={!nodePrompt.trim() || executeNode.isPending}
> 357	                className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-emerald-600 hover:from-green-700 hover:to-emerald-700 gap-2"
> 358	                size="lg"
> 359	              >
> 360	                {executeNode.isPending ? (
> 361	                  <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
> 362	                ) : (
> 363	                  <Play className="h-4 w-4" />
> 364	                )}
> 365	                {executeNode.isPending ? "Generating..." : "Execute Node"}
> 366	              </Button>
> 367	
> 368	              {/* Output preview */}
> 369	              {selectedNode?.data?.outputUrl && (
> 370	                <div>
> 371	                  <label className="text-xs font-medium mb-1 block text-muted-foreground">Output</label>
> 372	                  <img
> 373	                    src={selectedNode.data.outputUrl}
> 374	                    alt="Generated output"
> 375	                    className="rounded-lg w-full border border-border/50"
> 376	                  />
> 377	                </div>
> 378	              )}
> 379	            </div>
> 380	          </SheetContent>
> 381	        </Sheet>
> 382	      </div>
> 383	    </>
> 384	  );
> 385	}
> 386	
> ```

## 👤 User

Então como eu devo iniciar uma novasessõa?

## 🤖 Claude


## 🤖 Claude

Próxima sessão é simples — o protocolo já está armado pra você. Três opções dependendo do que quer fazer:

---

## 🚀 Caminho recomendado (90% das vezes)

Em uma sessão nova, digite:

```
/handson
```

Isso dispara o **ShakeHands v3** que automaticamente:
1. Roda pre-flight (git log, status, TS, Docker, Chroma)
2. Lê HANDOFF.md + CLAUDE.md + sprint-priorities.md + MEMORY.md
3. **NOVO v3:** valida completude das BoK suites em `docs/bok/`
4. Gera brief estruturado em PT-BR com:
   - Estado atual (fase selada: v6.0.0)
   - Últimos commits (16d4cc1, eb4b0cd, 721b68d…)
   - Infra, TypeScript, mesh nodes
   - **Próximos passos** já priorizados:
     1. Sprint 4 Canvas UX Mood Board (LanesOverlay + LeftPanel + StatusBar + TopBar + 4 node types)
     2. Phase Commercial (Stripe live)

Em ~30s você tem contexto completo sem queimar tokens em exploração.

---

## ⚡ Atalho direto pro Sprint 4

Se já quiser pular pra implementação sem o briefing completo:

```
/handson
Vamos para o Sprint 4 — Canvas UX Mood Board Completion. 
Começa pela Phase 4.1: portar LanesOverlay + LeftPanel + StatusBar + TopBar 
de /home/gcrUX/htdocs/viralmind-builder/src/components/canvas/ 
adaptando para vm_canvas_* (não vm_canvas_documents — usar projects/assets/executions/recipes).
```

Com isso a sessão arranca já com mira no entregável.

---

## 🎯 Para tarefas pontuais (não Sprint 4)

Se aparecer algo novo (bug, ideia, feature não documentada):

```
/handson
<descrever o que precisa>
```

Eu vou:
- Checar se a tarefa cabe em algum BoK existente
- Se for módulo novo significativo → alertar pra rodar `/bok-scribe` primeiro (gate v3)
- Se for fix/refactor/typo → executar direto

---

## 💡 Dicas de eficiência

| Situação | Comando ideal |
|----------|---------------|
| Continuar Sprint 4 | `/handson` + "Phase 4.1" |
| Investigar bug em prod | `/handson` + "Bug em <feature>: <descrição>" |
| Nova feature/módulo | `/handson` + "Quero criar X" → eu vou pedir `/bok-scribe` antes |
| Apenas verificar saúde | `/handson` (vê o brief e decide) |
| Iniciar nova auditoria 4Cs | `/handson` + `/audit` |

---

## 🛟 Backup disponível

Se algo der errado nessa sessão (improvável — push fast-forward limpo), tem branch local:
```bash
git checkout backup/pre-rebase-20260514
```
Volta pro ponto exato antes da reconciliação git desta sessão.

---

**Resumo executivo:** `/handson` é tudo que você precisa. O sistema já sabe o que fazer.
